Result: 在HRF数据集上使用自动生成的血管掩膜达到91.1%准确率，与RETFound（在大规模视网膜眼底数据上预训练的视觉Transformer）在相同评估设置下性能持平；三个公开眼底数据集均取得强分类表现。进一步分析表明预训练图像模型对采集相关空间线索（眼底尺度、视野内视网膜位置）及更广泛的非血管图像特征敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架支持无需大规模任务特定训练数据的可解释疾病分类、定量视网膜表型分析和视网膜生物标志物发现，同时为预训练模型的可解释性和潜在偏倚提供了新见解。

Abstract: Retinal fundus photography is widely used for screening and monitoring ocular diseases, but many modern classification pipelines rely on deep latent representations and provide limited interpretability. This study develops an interpretable fundus image classification framework based on a ring-structured representation of the retinal vasculature centered on the optic disc. The method quantifies vessel geometry, color appearance, oxygenation-related vascular appearance, and vessel--background entropy within concentric retinal regions. These physiologically motivated descriptors are derived from vessel masks, image intensities, and optical-density measurements and aggregated across rings to capture spatial variation in vascular properties. Using only quantitative vascular descriptors, the proposed method achieved strong classification performance across three public fundus datasets. On HRF, it achieved 91.1\% accuracy using automatically generated vessel masks, matching RETFound, a vision transformer pretrained on large-scale retinal fundus image data, under the same evaluation setting. Additional analyses suggest that pretrained image models are sensitive to acquisition-related spatial cues, including fundus scale and retinal position within the field of view, as well as broader non-vessel image characteristics. This framework may support interpretable disease classification, quantitative retinal phenotyping, and retinal biomarker discovery without requiring large task-specific training datasets.

</details>


### [86] [TorchMorph: CUDA-accelerated Morphological Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24738)
*Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TorchMorph是一个轻量级PyTorch扩展，将scipy.ndimage的形态学变换算子移植为直接操作CUDA张量的融合内核，支持最多8个空间维度、22个公开算子，性能最高可达scipy的1100倍，且API与scipy逐参数兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python生态中事实标准的形态学变换实现scipy.ndimage仅支持CPU、单数组操作，无法在GPU训练循环中直接使用（需要昂贵的设备到主机往返传输）。现有的基于PyTorch的GPU视觉库仅覆盖少数算子，通常局限于二维空间和扁平结构元素，存在明显的功能缺口。

Method: TorchMorph采用分层架构，设计并实现了多系列融合CUDA内核，覆盖二元形态学、灰度形态学、精确与近似距离变换、以及熵正则化最优传输共22个公开算子。API刻意逐参数镜像scipy.ndimage（包括边界模式、结构元素原点、预分配输出），实现现有管线的零成本迁移。所有算子直接操作(B, C, Spatial...)格式的CUDA张量，支持最多八个空间维度。

Result: 与单线程CPU参考实现对比，批量执行在灰度形态学上达到scipy.ndimage最高1100倍的吞吐量，精确欧氏距离变换最高350倍加速；Sinkhorn求解器比POT快42倍。二元和chamfer算子与SciPy输出完全一致，所有浮点算子与CPU参考的绝对误差不超过1.8e-6。

Conclusion: TorchMorph填补了GPU形态学算子在PyTorch生态中的空白，实现了与scipy.ndimage的API级兼容和高性能GPU加速，以MIT许可证开源发布，可作为现有视觉管线的即插即用替代方案。

Abstract: Morphological transforms are long-standing tools for shape and mask processing, but the de facto reference implementation in the Python ecosystem, i.e. scipy.ndimage, is CPU-only, single-array, and therefore unusable inside a GPU training loop without an expensive device-to-host round trip. GPU vision libraries built on PyTorch cover a narrow subset of these operators, typically restricted to two spatial dimensions and flat structuring elements. We present TorchMorph, a lightweight PyTorch extension that closes this gap. TorchMorph exposes 22 public operators covering binary morphology, greyscale morphology, exact and approximate distance transforms, and entropy-regularised optimal transport, all implemented as fused CUDA kernels that operate directly on (B, C, Spatial...) CUDA tensors with up to eight spatial dimensions. The API deliberately mirrors scipy.ndimage argument-for-argument, including border modes, structuring-element origins and pre-allocated outputs, so that existing pipelines port with a change of import. We describe the layered architecture and the kernel designs behind each operator family. Against single-threaded CPU references, batched execution reaches up to 1.1e3 times the throughput of scipy.ndimage on greyscale morphology and up to 350x on exact Euclidean distance transforms, while the Sinkhorn solver runs up to 42x faster than POT. Binary and chamfer operators reproduce their SciPy counterparts exactly, and every float-valued operator agrees with the CPU reference to within 1.8e-6 absolute error. TorchMorph is released under the MIT licence at https://intcomp.github.io/tm.

</details>


### [87] [Weakly Supervised Seafloor Segmentation for Seagrass Habitat Mapping in Side-Scan Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24756)
*Hayat Rajani,Nuno Gracias,Rafael Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将弱监督语义分割框架应用于侧扫声纳（SSS）海底栖息地制图的方法，仅使用图像级标签即可学习像素级海草草甸地图。该框架结合ViT编码器-解码器与分类分支、类激活图、稠密条件随机场伪标签细化，并采用迭代自训练和类别不平衡采样策略。在测试断面上的mIoU达87.6%，经自监督预训练后进一步提升3%，展示了在海岸尺度进行高效海草监测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海草草甸是重要的蓝色碳汇栖息地，绘制其分布对海岸管理和碳清单至关重要。光学卫星传感器覆盖范围大但无法探测深水或浑浊水域，而侧扫声纳虽可在任意深度高分辨率成像海底，但其解译仍依赖密集的人工标注，既缓慢又昂贵。因此需要开发一种仅需图像级标签即可完成像素级海草制图的方法。

Method: 将弱监督语义分割框架适配到侧扫声纳海底栖息地制图：(1) 采用基于ViT的编码器-解码器与分类分支结合；(2) 提取类激活图并通过针对声学图像噪声和弱边界调整的稠密条件随机场细化为伪标签；(3) 迭代自训练方案配合处理强类别不平衡的采样策略；(4) 评估不同损失函数，发现Lovász-Softmax损失最有效；(5) 在无标签SSS数据上进行自监督预训练以进一步提升性能。

Result: 在留出测试断面上，细化后的伪标签对真值达到89.3%的mIoU；完全无像素级标签训练的语义分割分支达到87.6%的mIoU；自监督预训练额外带来3%的mIoU提升。野外试验进一步验证了模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于侧扫声纳的准确且标签高效的弱监督海底栖息地制图在海岸尺度海草监测所需的规模上是可行的，为大规模蓝碳栖息地制图提供了高效且成本更低的替代方案。

Abstract: Seagrass meadows are crucial blue-carbon habitats, and mapping their extent is a prerequisite for coastal management and carbon inventory. Optical satellite sensors cover large areas but cannot reach deep or turbid water, whereas side-scan sonar (SSS) images the seabed at high resolution and at any depth. Interpreting SSS, however, still relies on dense manual annotation, which is slow and costly. We address this by adapting a weakly supervised semantic segmentation framework to SSS benthic habitat mapping, so that pixel-level maps are learned from image-level labels alone. The framework couples a ViT-based encoder-decoder with a classification branch, extracts class activation maps, and refines them into pseudo-labels with a dense conditional random field that we tune for the noise and weak boundaries of acoustic imagery. It follows an iterative self-training scheme, together with a sampling strategy to cope with the strong class imbalance of the data. We also study the effect of different loss functions on segmentation quality, finding Lovász-Softmax loss the most effective. On a held-out transect, the refined pseudo-labels reached an mIoU of 89.3\% against the ground truth, and the segmentation branch, trained without any pixel-level labels, reached 87.6\%. Self-supervised pretraining on unlabelled SSS added a further 3\% in mean intersection-over-union. Field trials further demonstrate the generalizability of the trained model. These results show that accurate and label-efficient benthic habitat mapping from side-scan sonar is feasible at the scale needed for coast-wide seagrass monitoring.

</details>


### [88] [DiD It in 87 Minutes: A Label-Free Softmax-to-Linear Adaptation of Vision Transformers for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22368)
*Huaiyuan Qin,Gabriel James Goenawan,Zihang Lin,Muli Yang,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出检测器接口蒸馏（DiD），一种无需标签的架构转换方法，通过对齐冻结的Softmax教师与线性注意力学生骨干之间的检测器接口张量，将已训练好的Softmax注意力检测器骨干转换为线性注意力版本。在DOTA-v1.5上，DiD显著优于基线方法，匹配完全监督训练模型，同时将推理延迟降低约62%、峰值内存降低约49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力因其降低全局token混合成本而成为高分辨率目标检测的有力机制，但将已训练检测器中的Softmax注意力ViT骨干直接替换为线性注意力并非简单的替代方案——直接交换注意力算子会导致严重性能下降，而通用的无标签蒸馏（对分类有效）在检测任务上往往失败。核心挑战在于检测器接口保持：转换后的骨干必须复现固定下游检测器所期望的精确特征张量，而不仅仅是模仿内部的Softmax隐藏状态。

Method: 提出DiD（Detector-Interface Distillation），一种无需标签的转换方法，仅训练线性注意力骨干，通过将检测器面对层的接口张量与冻结的Softmax教师对齐，而非直接模仿中间隐藏状态。该方法专注训练骨干，保持下游检测器固定不变。

Result: 在DOTA-v1.5数据集上，DiD显著优于已有基线方法，并匹配完全监督训练的线性模型。整个适配过程约87分钟完成于4块GPU上。线性化骨干将推理延迟降低约62%，峰值内存降低约49%。

Conclusion: DiD为社区提供了一条简单、无需标签的路径，将已训练的Softmax检测器复用为高效的线性检测器，并鼓励未来架构转换工作采用接口感知的目标函数。

Abstract: While linear attention is a compelling mechanism for high-resolution object detection due to its reduced cost for global token mixing, converting the Softmax-attention ViT backbone of a trained detector into a linear-attention one is not a trivial drop-in replacement. Directly swapping the attention operator leads to severe performance degradation, and generic label-free distillation, though effective for classification, often fails on detection tasks. We argue that the central challenge is \textit{detector-interface preservation}: the converted backbone must reproduce the exact feature tensors expected by the fixed downstream detector, rather than merely imitating internal Softmax hidden states. To address this, we introduce Detector-Interface Distillation (DiD), a label-free conversion method that exclusively trains the linear-attention backbone by aligning detector-facing interface tensors with those of a frozen Softmax teacher. On DOTA-v1.5, DiD substantially outperforms established baselines and matches supervised, fully trained linear models. Adaptation completes in roughly 87 minutes on 4 GPUs, and the linearized backbone cuts inference latency by ~62% and peak memory by ~49%. We hope our findings offer the community a simple, label-free route to reusing trained Softmax detectors as efficient linear ones, and encourage interface-aware objectives in future architecture-conversion work.

</details>


### [89] [IDeaL: Data-Free Multi-Teacher Distillation via Improved Dead Leaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24759)
*Feyza Yavuz,Mert Bülent Sarıyıldız,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据免费的多教师蒸馏方法IDeaL，通过为每个教师生成定制化的改进噪声样本（而非依赖真实训练数据），利用补丁级和图像级的去相关损失来优化样本，使学生模型能捕捉所有教师的互补信息，在1K图像预算下效果媲美甚至超越使用ImageNet子集蒸馏的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多教师蒸馏通常假设可以获得教师训练数据的并集来训练学生模型，但这一假设在现实中往往不成立。作者质疑这一假设，并探索无需真实数据时的替代方案，希望在不访问原始训练数据的情况下仍能实现有效的多教师知识融合。

Method: 首先研究从接收不同类型噪声的教师进行蒸馏能达到的效果；提出利用教师模型本身携带的信息来定制化生成每个教师专属的改进噪声样本；通过补丁级和图像级两个层面的去相关损失，优化生成适合无数据多教师蒸馏的样本，形成IDeaL方法。

Result: 实验表明IDeaL方法生成的最有效样本能让学生模型成功捕捉教师的互补信息，取得极具竞争力的结果，大幅缩小了与使用真实图像蒸馏的学生模型之间的差距；在1K图像预算下，基于IDeaL样本蒸馏的学生模型匹配或超越了使用ImageNet 1K图像子集蒸馏的性能。

Conclusion: 多教师蒸馏无需依赖教师模型的原始训练数据也能取得接近甚至超越真实数据蒸馏的效果。通过教师自身的知识来定制化生成训练样本，为无数据环境下的知识蒸馏提供了可行且高效的新路径。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation has emerged as a way to combine complementary teacher models into a single student model that exhibits the strengths of all its teachers. The student is trained to mimic the output of the teachers on a set of images, typically the union of the individual teacher's training sets, assuming this data is available. In this paper, we question that assumption and explore alternative options. We first study how far one can go when distilling from teachers fed with different types of noise. Then, we show that information contained in the teachers can be leveraged to tailor the noise for multi-teacher distillation: we propose a method that, thanks to decorrelation losses at both patch and image levels, generates teacher-specific, improved samples optimized for data-free distillation. Experiments show that our most effective samples, IDeaL, lead to strong students that successfully capture complementary information from the teachers, yielding surprisingly competitive results that substantially narrow the gap with students distilled from real images. Moreover, given a limited budget of 1K images for distillation, students distilled using our IDeaL samples match or surpass the performance of those distilled using a 1K-image subset of ImageNet.

</details>


### [90] [MoTE: Mixture of Task Experts for Multi-Task Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24763)
*Muhammad Asad Ali,Umar Khan,Nadia Robertini,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoTE（Mixture of Task Experts），一种将大语言模型前馈网络转换为任务专属专家的解码器架构，通过样本级任务路由实现多任务视频语言学习，在五个COIN基准上以约20亿参数激活达到更高平均top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过程性视频语言模型需要从相同的视觉证据中解决异构任务（动作识别、预测、过程预测等），但密集Transformer解码器在不同任务间共享同一套前馈网络，导致任务行为互相纠缠且难以实现可控的能力扩展。稀疏MoE解码器虽提供条件计算，但token级学习路由与任务级过程目标不对齐。

Method: 提出MoTE解码器架构：将LLM中的前馈网络转换为任务专属专家，同时保持多模态骨干网络共享。每个样本遵循一条样本级任务路由，使活跃任务专家的计算量独立于存储的任务专家数量。实例化为VideoLLM-MoTE，使用显式任务路由在五个COIN基准上进行评估。

Result: 五专家模型每样本激活约20亿LLM参数，平均top-1准确率超过近期VideoLLM基线。在相同专家拓扑下，优于密集全专家激活和学习的稀疏路由控制方法。

Conclusion: 任务结构化路由为多任务视频语言学习提供了可解释且计算高效的解码器替代方案。

Abstract: Procedural video-language models must solve heterogeneous tasks from the same visual evidence, including action recognition, forecasting, and procedure prediction. Dense transformer decoders share the same feed-forward networks across tasks, which can entangle task behavior and make controlled capability expansion difficult. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoders provide conditional computation, but token-level learned routing is not naturally aligned with task-level procedural objectives. We propose MoTE (Mixture of Task Experts), a decoder architecture that converts large language model feed-forward networks into task-specific experts while keeping the multimodal backbone shared. Each example follows one sample-level task route, so active task-expert computation remains independent of the number of stored task experts. We instantiate this design as VideoLLM-MoTE and evaluate it on five COIN benchmarks using explicit task routes. The five-expert model activates ~2B LLM parameters per sample and achieves higher average top-1 accuracy than recent VideoLLM baselines. Under the same expert topology, it improves over dense all-expert activation and learned sparse-routing controls. These results show that task-structured routing provides an interpretable and compute-efficient decoder alternative for multi-task video-language learning.

</details>


### [91] [Ensemble of Convolutional Neural Networks for StrokePrediction: Towards Improved Diagnostic Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24771)
*Md Shahriar Sajid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于机器学习的脑卒中智能预测系统，使用11个特征，通过7种监督学习算法进行对比评估。集成方法（随机森林、Stacking、Bagging）达到99.52%的最高准确率，证明了集成学习在脑卒中分类中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中具有高死亡率和高发病率，早期诊断和预防措施能大幅减少生命损失和残疾。传统诊断手段难以满足快速干预需求，深度学习的发展为计算机辅助早期卒中检测提供了新机遇。

Method: 使用11个特征建立智能预测系统，采用7种监督学习算法（随机森林、Stacking、Bagging、决策树、KNN、TabNet、自定义前馈网络、SVC和逻辑回归）进行对比。流程包括文献综述、数据集可视化、数据预处理和模型评估。

Result: 集成方法（随机森林、Stacking、Bagging）达到99.52%的高准确率；决策树98.24%；KNN 96.73%；TabNet 96.49%；自定义前馈模型94.91%；SVC和逻辑回归较低，分别为88.06%和77.03%。

Conclusion: 集成方法在脑卒中分类任务中表现最优，验证了其在脑卒中早期检测中的有效性，为该领域计算机辅助诊断提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Brain stroke, known for its high mortality and incidence rates, poses significant health risks and requires rapid intervention for survival. Early diagnosis and preventive measures can greatly reduce life loss and disabilities. Recent advancements in deep learning have led to novel computer-aided diagnostic techniques for early stroke detection. This study proposes an intelligent system that predicts potential strokes using eleven features, evaluated through seven supervised machine learning algorithms. The process includes a literature review, dataset visualization, data preprocessing, and model evaluation. Ensemble methods like Random Forest, Stacking Classifier, and Bagging Classifier achieved high accuracies of 99.52%, while Decision Tree reached 98.24%. Other models, including KNN and TabNet, demonstrated reliable performance, achieving accuracies of 96.73% and 96.49%, respectively. The custom feedforward model achieved 94.91%, while SVC and logistic regression had lower accuracies at 88.06% and 77.03%. The results highlight the effectiveness of ensemble methods in stroke classification.

</details>


### [92] [Image Difference Quantification Using Autoencoder-Based Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24782)
*Manish Sharma,Timothy Yim,Clifton Forlines*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于卷积自编码器的框架，在潜在空间中利用余弦相似度衡量图像差异，克服传统像素级指标（MSE、PSNR、SSIM）无法捕捉感知意义的局限。在狗猫图像及跨域数据集上验证了清晰的类间可分性，并在TID2013数据集上显示与人类MOS评分正相关，为内容检索和质量评估提供了高效且语义化的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像相似度度量（MSE、PSNR、SSIM）基于像素级比较，难以捕捉人类感知上真正有意义的图像差异；而深度神经网络学到的潜在表示编码了更符合人类视觉感知的高层语义信息，因此有必要探索基于潜在空间的图像相似度度量方法。

Method: 提出一种基于卷积自编码器的框架，将图像映射到紧凑的潜在嵌入空间，并利用余弦相似度量化图像间的差异。该方法利用深度网络学习的高层语义表示，在光照、姿态和背景等变化条件下实现对视觉上不同图像的鲁棒区分。

Result: 在狗猫图像及跨域数据集上，潜在空间中呈现清晰的类内聚类和强类间可分性；98.4%的狗猫图像对相似度得分低于0.5。在TID2013数据集上，潜在空间距离与人类平均意见得分（MOS）正相关，展示了对感知相关图像失真的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种计算高效且具有语义基础的图像相似度度量方式，可作为传统像素级指标的替代方案，在基于内容的检索、感知质量评估和语义相似性分析等领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Traditional image similarity metrics such as Mean Squared Error (MSE), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and the Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) rely on pixel-level comparisons and often fail to capture perceptually meaningful differences between images. In contrast, latent representations learned by deep neural networks encode high-level semantic information that is more closely aligned with human visual perception. This paper proposes a convolutional autoencoder-based framework for quantifying image differences using cosine similarity in latent space. The learned compact embeddings enable robust differentiation between visually distinct images under variations in illumination, pose, and background. Extensive evaluation on dog-cat images and additional cross-domain datasets demonstrates clear class-wise clustering and strong inter-class separability in the latent space, with 98.4% of dog-cat image pairs exhibiting similarity scores below 0.5. Further validation using the TID2013 dataset shows that latent-space distance correlates positively with human Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), demonstrating sensitivity to perceptually relevant image distortions. The proposed approach provides a computationally efficient and semantically grounded alternative to conventional pixel-based similarity metrics, with potential applications in content-based retrieval, perceptual quality assessment, and semantic similarity analysis.

</details>


### [93] [MoE-based Feature Adapter for Prompt-free Binary Coronary Artery Segmentation in X-ray Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24783)
*Lin Xi,Yingliang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无提示（prompt-free）混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）特征适配器的冠状动脉分割方法，用于X射线血管造影视频中的二值冠脉分割。该方法在参数高效的Vision Transformer适配器基础上，使用多个轻量级专家结合输入依赖的top-k路由机制，自适应地细化血管相关特征，在MOSXAV和XACV数据集上优于代表性基线，并提升了跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线血管造影视频中的冠状动脉精确分割对定量分析和图像引导干预至关重要，但冠脉血管细且对比度低，加之导管、导丝和复杂解剖背景的干扰，分割难度大。现有U-Net和Transformer模型虽提供了强基线，但其共享的特征适应路径难以应对异质的血管造影外观，需引入更灵活的自适应特征适配机制。

Method: 提出无提示的MoE特征适配器，构建于参数高效的Vision Transformer适配器之上。采用多个轻量级专家，通过输入依赖的top-k路由机制动态选择激活的专家，自适应地细化血管相关特征，同时控制活跃计算成本，避免引入人工提示信息。

Result: 在MOSXAV数据集上的内部实验及XACV数据集上的外部评估显示，所提方法优于U-Net和Transformer等代表性基线，并显著改善了跨数据集泛化性能。

Conclusion: MoE-based适配器学习对X射线血管造影视频中的鲁棒冠状动脉分割是有效的，为处理异质血管造影外观提供了一种有前景的范式。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of coronary arteries in X-ray angiography videos is essential for quantitative coronary analysis and image-guided interventions. However, accurate segmentation remains challenging because coronary vessels are thin and exhibit low contrast, while the presence of catheters, guidewires, and complex anatomical background structures can further interfere with vessel delineation. Existing U-Net- and Transformer-based models provide strong baselines, but their shared feature-adaptation pathways may be insufficient for heterogeneous angiographic appearances. In this paper, we propose a prompt-free mixture-of-experts (MoE) feature adapter for binary coronary artery segmentation. Built upon parameter-efficient Vision Transformer adapters, the proposed method uses multiple lightweight experts with input-dependent top-$k$ routing to adaptively refine vessel-related features while limiting active computational cost. Experiments on MOSXAV and external evaluation on XACV show that the proposed method outperforms representative baselines and improves cross-dataset generalisation. These results suggest that MoE-based adapter learning is effective for robust coronary artery segmentation in X-ray angiography videos.

</details>


### [94] [EMFE: A lightweight, explainable machine learning framework for malaria cell classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24793)
*Md Abdullah Al Kafi,Walayat Hussain,Mousumi Karmakar,Sumit Kumar Banshal,Ahmed Al Marouf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMFE（高效数学特征提取）框架，仅用五个手工特征结合经典机器学习分类疟疾红细胞图像，在NIH疟疾数据集上达到94.6%的准确率，与深度学习模型竞争的同时提供可解释性和极低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度卷积神经网络在疟疾自动诊断中虽准确但计算开销大、可解释性差、且缺乏患者级别的统计严谨性验证。本文旨在提供一个轻量级、可解释、且经过严格统计学验证的替代方案。

Method: 五步特征提取流程：Gray World颜色归一化、自适应绿色通道阈值分割、形态学斑点检测，然后提取五个手工特征；使用三种经典机器学习分类器（随机森林、直方图梯度提升、支持向量机）进行分类；采用患者分组嵌套交叉验证（外10折/内3折）确保同患者细胞不跨折；并进行消融实验、硬件匹配的深度学习对比、合成扰动表征失效模式、可解释性分析及患者级聚合分析。

Result: 优化后的随机森林达到94.6%池化折外准确率（95%CI [93.6, 95.7]），40患者留出集确认94.3%，患者级置换检验p<0.001；消融实验量化各特征与流水线阶段的贡献；与DenseNet121、ResNet50、MobileNetV2的硬件匹配对比评估了准确率-效率权衡；识别出三种失效模式和斑点饱和作为主导判别特征。

Conclusion: EMFE证明了在统计学严格、可解释且计算轻量条件下，经典特征工程加机器学习可以作为深度学习的有效替代方案，同时明确量化了其局限性，为资源受限场景下的疟疾诊断提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Automated malaria diagnosis from stained blood-smear microscopy is dominated by deep convolutional neural networks that are accurate but computationally expensive, poorly interpretable, and rarely validated with patient-level rigor. We present EMFE (Efficient Mathematical Feature Extraction), a five-feature framework for classifying single red-blood-cell images as parasitized or uninfected using Gray World color normalization, adaptive green-channel thresholding, morphological spot detection, and classical machine learning. Using the NIH LHNCBC malaria dataset (27,558 images from 200 patients), we evaluate Random Forest, Histogram Gradient Boosting, and Support Vector Machine classifiers under patient-grouped nested cross-validation (K_outer=20, K_inner=3), ensuring that cells from each patient remain within a single fold. The optimized Random Forest achieves 94.6% pooled out-of-fold accuracy (95% CI [93.6, 95.7]), corroborated by an untouched 40-patient holdout test (94.3%) and a patient-level permutation test (p<0.001, 1,000 permutations). Ablation experiments quantify the contribution of individual features and pipeline stages. Hardware-matched comparisons with retrained DenseNet121, ResNet50, and MobileNetV2 models assess the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Synthetic perturbations characterize three failure modes, while explainability analysis identifies spot saturation as the dominant discriminative feature. Patient-level aggregation further quantifies sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and false-positive accumulation. These results demonstrate a statistically rigorous, interpretable, and computationally lightweight alternative to deep learning, while explicitly quantifying its limitations.

</details>


### [95] [LAION-BVD: A 10-Million-Hour Open Video Dataset for Multimodal Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24845)
*Andreas Hochlehnert,Marianna Nezhurina,Mehdi Cherti,Andrej Radonjic,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Christoph Schuhmann,Romain Beaumont,Wieland Brendel,Bernhard Schölkopf,A. Sophia Koepke,Jenia Jitsev,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAION-BVD是一个大规模开放视频数据集，包含从CommonCrawl收集的13亿视频URL，实际下载8000万视频（总时长1000万小时），支持视频、音频、图像三种模态的多模态预训练。通过内容感知场景检测提取片段并合成视频和音频字幕，在该数据上训练的模型在视频-文本和音频-文本基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态预训练数据集在规模和多模态覆盖方面存在局限，尤其缺乏开放的大规模视频数据资源。作者旨在构建前所未有的超大规模开放视频数据集，以推动多模态学习研究。

Method: 从CommonCrawl收集13亿平台特定视频URL；实际下载8000万视频，总时长1000万小时；采用内容感知场景检测提取片段；为片段合成生成视频和音频字幕；同时提取场景变化帧作为图像-文本数据的替代来源。

Result: 在该数据集上训练的模型在标准视频-文本和音频-文本基准测试中达到有竞争力的性能，且随训练或模型规模增加持续改进。场景变化帧展现出与标准网页图像语料库不同的视觉分布，基于该数据训练的模型在图像-文本检索任务上表现强劲。

Conclusion: LAION-BVD显著扩展了开放多模态视频数据的可访问性，以空前的规模为研究社区提供资源，开辟了多模态预训练的新可能性。

Abstract: We present LAION-BVD, a large-scale open video dataset for multimodal learning, which contains 1.3B platform-specific video URLs collected from CommonCrawl. From these, we download 80M videos with a total duration of 10 million hours. The dataset is designed for multimodal pre-training across the video, audio, and image modalities. Using content-aware scene detection, we extract clips for which we synthetically generate video and audio captions. Models trained on these data achieve competitive performance on standard video-text and audio-text benchmarks, with consistent improvements as training or model scale increases. Additionally, we explore video frames as an alternative source of image-text data by extracting scene-changing frames. These frames exhibit a visual distribution distinct from standard web image corpora, and models trained on this dataset achieve strong image-text retrieval performance. We release LAION-BVD to the research community. It significantly expands open access to multimodal videos at an unprecedented scale.

</details>


### [96] [LeFlow: Generative Latent Flow Planning for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24855)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Junbin Lu,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeFlow提出在学习隐式世界模型后，将规划本身摊销化——利用整流流模型在潜在动力学空间中生成条件潜在轨迹，再通过逆动力学解码器将其转换为动作块，由冻结的世界模型验证，取代传统的迭代式动作空间优化，在四个目标条件像素控制基准上实现成功率提升并使规划时间降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型虽然本身是强编码器（将像素映射到潜在嵌入），但动作规划仍依赖在线轨迹优化：每个状态-目标对都需从零运行迭代优化器搜索最优动作序列，这会重复付出完整迭代优化成本且不跨查询复用规划经验。

Method: LeFlow学习可复用的潜在轨迹先验，直接在来自世界模型的潜在动力学空间中运行。核心包括：(1) 使用整流流（rectified-flow）模型将规划重构为条件潜在轨迹生成，在当前的嵌入与目标嵌入之间想象未来潜在路径；(2) 逆动力学解码器将潜在转移转换为动作块；(3) 冻结的世界模型通过自回归推演验证每个候选，采用固定预算的rollout选择。

Result: 在四个主要目标条件像素控制基准上，LeFlow用摊销的潜在规划与固定预算rollout选择替代迭代动作空间优化，取得一致的成功率提升，并且规划时间减少一个数量级（约10倍）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明潜在世界模型不仅应支持预测，还应支持可复用的规划先验。代码已开源。

Abstract: Latent world models are inherently strong encoders that transform image pixel to latent embedding, yet existing world models still rely on online trajectory optimization for action planning: for every state-goal pair, an iterative optimizer is run from scratch to search for optimal action sequences, treating the world model as a black-box simulator. This approach pays the full iterative optimization cost anew at every replanning step and reuses no planning experience across queries. In this work, we ask whether planning itself can be amortized once a latent world model has been learned. We present LeFlow, which learns a reusable latent trajectory prior operating directly in the latent dynamics space from the world model. LeFlow recasts planning as conditional latent trajectory generation: a rectified-flow model imagines a future latent path between the current and goal embeddings, an inverse dynamics decoder turns latent transitions into action chunks, and the frozen world model verifies each candidate by autoregressive rollout. Across four major goal-conditioned pixel-control benchmarks, LeFlow replaces iterative action-space optimization with amortized latent planning and fixed-budget rollout selection, achieving consistent success-rate gains with an order-of-magnitude reduction in planning time. Our results argue that latent world models should support not only prediction but reusable planning priors. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/LeFlow.

</details>


### [97] [From Seeing to Acting: Smart Glasses as First-Person Intelligence Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24877)
*Jiangning Zhang,Haojun Chen,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是首个以统一框架系统研究智能眼镜的综述，提出感知-状态-交互-动作闭环的第一人称智能范式，涵盖硬件能力轴、七项基础能力、L0-L5分级、九大应用场景、部署框架与评估协议，为智能眼镜的可比较性、可部署性和可复现评估提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜正从捕获与显示配件演变为第一人称智能平台，但其在能量、热、隐私和反馈约束下运行需要完整系统级能力；现有文献在设备、任务和基准上碎片化，核心挑战在于能否维持可靠、时间有效、可纠错、可治理的感知-状态-交互-动作闭环，而并非单个模型孤立地识别、回答、记忆或行动。因此需要一个统一框架来系统化研究。

Method: 采用统一框架方法：1) 形式化第一人称数据流与受约束任务效用；2) 沿八条可验证硬件能力轴刻画设备；3) 围绕七项相互依赖的基础能力组织文献；4) 引入L0-L5分级框架（捕获、反应感知、上下文辅助、持久状态、治理动作、具身耦合）；5) 跨九个应用场景连接任务与数据集、系统、产品、利益相关者、失败后果和证据缺口；6) 提出九维部署框架、声明条件化评估协议及从受控测量到纵向现场验证和审计的证据阶梯。

Result: 构建了完整的智能眼镜研究分类体系：包括八条硬件能力轴、七项基础能力、L0-L5成熟度分级、九大应用场景的映射关系、九维部署框架、声明条件化评估协议和证据阶梯，使智能眼镜领域更加可比较、可部署、可复现评估。

Conclusion: 智能眼镜作为第一人称智能平台的核心在于系统级闭环能力而非单体模型性能。通过统一的分类框架、分级体系和评估协议，能够提升该领域的可比较性与可部署性，并为构建值得信赖的第一人称智能指明路线图。未来方向包括从受控测量向纵向现场验证与审计的证据方法演进。

Abstract: Smart glasses are evolving from capture and display accessories into first-person intelligence platforms that connect human perception, persistent context, and digital or physical action. Their on-body viewpoint aligns with the wearer's vision, audition, motion, and hand-object interaction, but must operate under tight energy, thermal, privacy, and feedback constraints. Despite rapid progress in augmented reality, egocentric vision, multimodal models, human-computer interaction, and embodied intelligence, the literature remains fragmented across devices, tasks, and benchmarks. \textit{The key challenge is not whether a model can recognize, answer, remember, or act in isolation, but whether a complete system can sustain a reliable, temporally valid, correctable, and governable perception-state-interaction-action loop.} This survey is \textit{the \textbf{first} to systematically study smart glasses through such a unified framework}. We formalize first-person data flow and constrained task utility, characterize devices along eight verifiable hardware capability axes, organize the literature around seven interdependent foundational capabilities, and introduce an L0-L5 framework spanning capture, reactive perception, contextual assistance, persistent state, governed action, and embodied coupling. Across nine application scenes, we connect tasks with datasets, systems, products, stakeholders, failure consequences, and evidence gaps. We further present a nine-dimensional deployment framework, a claim-conditioned evaluation protocol, and an evidence ladder from controlled measurement to longitudinal field validation and audit. Together, these elements make smart glasses more comparable, deployable, and reproducibly evaluated, while outlining a roadmap toward trustworthy first-person intelligence.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Taming Visual Neglect: A Variational Information Bottleneck Framework for Adaptive Attention in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23570)
*Kaito Tanaka,Yuji Nishimura,Keisuke Matsuda,Aya Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VIB-ICL框架，通过信息瓶颈原理和跨模态信息增益（CMIG）解释了多模态上下文学习（ICL）中视觉上下文何时以及为何起作用，并推导出泛化界和注意力重分配原则，实验证明该方法在五个基准上一致提升准确率并减少所需示范数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型展现出强上下文学习能力，但视觉上下文在何时以及为何帮助多模态ICL仍不清楚。经验研究显示一个令人困惑的二分现象：模型有时有效利用视觉示范，有时却完全忽视它们。这种不一致的行为缺乏统一的理论解释。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原理提出VIB-ICL框架。具体包括：(1) 提出跨模态信息增益（CMIG）度量视觉上下文提供的关于目标token的额外互信息；(2) 推导泛化界，证明多模态ICL的超出文本ICL的额外风险由CMIG控制；(3) 证明当视觉信息冗余时，视觉上下文忽视是信息瓶颈最优解，从而得出封闭形式的注意力重分配原则；(4) 通过变分界估计CMIG并动态重新分配注意力权重。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验显示，VIB-ICL带来最高4.7%的准确率提升和35%的示范数量减少，验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作从信息论视角统一解释了多模态ICL中视觉上下文的"利用"与"忽视"这一二分现象：当视觉信息非冗余时利用是有益的，当视觉信息冗余时忽视是信息瓶颈最优解。理论保证了多模态ICL的优越性，并通过注意力重分配算法将理论转化为实际性能提升。

Abstract: Large vision-language models exhibit strong in-context learning (ICL) capabilities, yet when and why visual context helps multimodal ICL remains poorly understood. Empirical studies show a puzzling dichotomy: models sometimes effectively leverage visual demonstrations, yet often neglect them entirely. We propose VIB-ICL, an information-theoretic framework that resolves this dichotomy through the Information Bottleneck principle. We introduce the Cross-Modal Information Gain (CMIG), which quantifies the additional mutual information that visual context provides about the target beyond textual context. We derive a generalization bound showing that multimodal ICL's excess risk over text-only ICL is governed by the CMIG, proving that multimodal ICL provably outperforms text-only ICL when visual information is non-redundant. We further prove that visual context neglect, often viewed as a failure mode, is the Information Bottleneck-optimal solution when visual information is redundant, yielding a closed-form Attention Reallocation Principle that prescribes how visual attention weights should be adaptively adjusted. We instantiate this principle in the VIB-ICL algorithm, which estimates CMIG via variational bounds and dynamically reallocates attention. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 4.7\% accuracy gains and 35\% reduction in required demonstrations, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [99] [From Triage to Discharge: A Survey of NLP Tasks, Methods, and Open Challenges in the Emergency Department](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23627)
*Dipankar Srirag,Aditya Joshi,Salil Kanhere,Padmanesan Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了46篇面向急诊科（ED）临床工作流的自然语言处理（NLP）研究论文，涵盖分诊、诊断和处置三个关键阶段，涉及分诊分类、临床摘要、自动诊断、报告生成和出院文档等任务，并总结了从特定任务神经网络到预训练语言模型的范式转变、交互式临床系统兴起以及临床接地评估等趋势，同时指出泛化性有限、临床输入噪声和工作流约束等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科在高时间压力下产生大量多模态数据（如临床对话、分诊笔记、出院文档），NLP和预训练大语言模型为这些语言密集和时间敏感环节提供了新的支持机会。然而现有综述要么覆盖整个医院流程过于宽泛，要么聚焦特定单一任务，缺少针对急诊科全流程NLP工作的系统梳理，因此需要一篇专门分析ED三阶段NLP研究的综述。

Method: 系统分析46篇论文，按急诊科三个流程阶段（分诊、诊断、处置）组织，覆盖分诊分类、临床摘要、自动诊断、报告生成、出院文档等任务；从建模范式、评估实践、新兴基准和共享任务三个维度进行归纳与对比分析。

Result: 识别出三个共同趋势：（1）建模范式从任务特定的神经架构转向预训练语言模型；（2）交互式临床系统日益受到关注；（3）评估实践愈发注重临床接地性（clinically grounded evaluation）。同时指出三大开放挑战：模型泛化能力有限、临床输入存在噪声（noisy inputs）、以及急诊工作流约束对NLP系统部署的影响。

Conclusion: 急诊科NLP正处于从通用预训练模型到临床专用、流程感知的交互式系统的演进阶段。未来研究需聚焦提升模型跨机构泛化能力、增强对噪声临床输入的鲁棒性，并充分考虑急诊工作流的时间与资源约束，以推动ED-NLP的实际临床落地。本文为后续研究提供了系统性的任务图谱和挑战清单。

Abstract: Emergency departments (EDs) operate under time pressure, generating multimodal data such as clinical conversations, triage notes, and discharge documents. Recent advances in natural language processing (NLP), particularly pretrained transformers and large language models, have created new opportunities to support language and time-intensive stages of emergency care. Yet existing surveys map clinical NLP across the broader hospital workflow or focus on specific tasks. This survey analyses 46 papers spanning the three phases of ED: triage, diagnosis, and disposition, covering tasks such as triage classification, clinical summarisation, automatic diagnosis, report generation, and discharge documentation. We examine modelling paradigms, evaluation practices, and emerging benchmarks and shared tasks. Across tasks, we identify common trends, including a shift from task-specific neural architectures to pretrained language models, growing interest in interactive clinical systems, and increasing attention to clinically grounded evaluation. Finally, we detail open challenges such as limited generalisability, noisy clinical inputs, and workflow constraints that inform future ED-NLP research.

</details>


### [100] [Contextual Embedding Evidence for Main--Light Verb Distinctions in Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23645)
*Farah Adeeba,Miriam Butt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用UrduBERT、DunbaaBERT和多语言BERT三种语境化嵌入模型，在包含七个乌尔都语动词的1,126个自然语句中检验乌尔都语轻动词与主要动词的表征差异。结果显示：主用与轻用在所有21个动词-模型组合中均表现出显著的表征分离；同词元主用和轻用质心比错配组合更接近，支持持续的词法关联性；在七分类预测任务中，目标词被遮蔽后动词身份仍可恢复（UrduBERT准确率0.866，宏F1 0.852），且在前后形式不重叠的评估中仍保持0.782的准确率，表明模型具备泛化能力。研究为Butt关于乌尔都语轻动词理论提供了计算层面的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语轻动词（light verbs）在语义上贡献图式性的事件结构意义，同时在词法上与其对应的主动词保持关联。本研究旨在通过语境化嵌入模型检验Butt理论中关于轻动词与主动词表征关系的可预测性假设，即二者应在表征上系统地区分，同时保留词元相关的结构信息。

Method: 使用UrduBERT、DunbaaBERT和multilingual BERT三种预训练语境化语言模型，对包含七个乌尔都语动词的1,126个自然语句进行嵌入分析。通过比较主用与轻用动词的表征分离度（21个动词-模型组合）、质心距离分析检验词元关联性，并在目标词遮蔽条件下执行七分类预测任务，同时设计了前置形式不重叠（preceding-form-disjoint）评估来测试泛化能力。

Result: 所有21个动词-模型组合中，主用和轻用表征均表现出显著分离；同词元的主用与轻用质心显著更近于错配组合；在七分类任务中，UrduBERT达到0.866准确率和0.852宏F1；在preceding-form-disjoint评估下保留0.782准确率，证明模型超越了局部重复动词组合的简单记忆。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了一致的计算证据，支持Butt关于乌尔都语轻动词的论断：轻动词与其主动词用法在表征上存在系统性差异，但同时也保留了词元特定（lemma-specific）和动词特定（verb-specific）的表征结构，验证了轻动词"图式性事件结构意义+词法关联"的双重理论属性。

Abstract: Urdu light verbs contribute schematic event-structural meaning while remaining lexically related to corresponding main verbs. This study tests representational predictions derived from Butt's analysis using contextual embeddings from UrduBERT, DunbaaBERT, and multilingual BERT across 1,126 naturally occurring sentences containing seven Urdu verbs. Main and light uses show significant representational separation in all 21 verb--model comparisons. At the same time, same-lemma main and light centroids are consistently closer than mismatched main--light lemma pairs, supporting continued lexical relatedness. In a seven-way prediction task restricted to light uses, verb identity remains recoverable after the target is masked, with UrduBERT achieving 0.866 accuracy and 0.852 macro-F1. UrduBERT also retains 0.782 accuracy under a preceding-form-disjoint evaluation, indicating generalization beyond repeated local verb combinations. These findings provide computational evidence consistent with Butt's account that Urdu light verbs differ systematically from their main uses while retaining lemma-specific and verb-specific representational structure.

</details>


### [101] [The Limits of Automatic Evaluation of Creativity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23705)
*Alessandro Tutone,Giorgio Franceschelli,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前自动评估方法能否可靠捕捉人类对创造力文本的判断。通过在WritingPrompts数据集上收集人类和AI生成短篇故事在11个创意维度上的人类评价，并对比客观自动指标与LLM-as-a-Judge评估，发现自动评估与人类判断存在严重错位：LLM评委系统性地偏爱AI生成故事，而广泛使用的自动指标与人类判断几乎零相关，揭示了当前创意文本自动评估方法的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在创造性文本生成方面越来越强，但评估其创造力仍是一大挑战。现有自动评估方法能否真实反映人类对创造力的判断尚不明确，需要系统验证当前评估工具的有效性。

Method: 收集WritingPrompts数据集中人类和AI生成的短篇故事在11个创造力维度上的人类评价，将其与自动化客观指标和LLM-as-a-Judge评估结果进行对比，并通过相关分析评估不同评估方法之间的一致性。

Result: 自动评估与人类判断之间存在显著错位。LLM评委对AI生成故事表现出系统性偏好，倾向于其风格特征而忽视人类文本的不可预测性等品质。广泛使用的自动指标与人类判断几乎为零相关，未能捕捉创造力的重要维度。

Conclusion: 当前创意文本自动评估方法存在根本性局限，创造力的多维性和主观性难以简化为计算指标，需要重新思考创造力评估的方法论。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of generating text that challenges human performance in domains requiring creativity, yet evaluating creativity in LLM-generated content remains a significant challenge. Here, we investigate whether current automatic evaluation methods can reliably capture human judgments of creativity. We collect human evaluations of human- and AI-generated short stories from the WritingPrompts dataset across 11 dimensions of creativity, and compare these judgments with automated objective metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments reveal substantial misalignment between automatic evaluations and human assessments. In particular, LLM-based judges exhibit a systematic preference for AI-generated stories, consistently favoring their stylistic characteristics over the unpredictability and other qualities of human-authored texts. Furthermore, correlation analyses show that widely used automatic metrics exhibit near-zero alignment with human judgments across both human- and AI-generated stories, suggesting that they fail to capture important dimensions of creativity. These findings highlight fundamental limitations in current approaches to the automatic evaluation of creative text and underscore the difficulty of reducing the multidimensional and subjective nature of creativity to computational metrics.

</details>


### [102] [ADE: Agentic Data Evolution Framework for Human-Centered Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23719)
*Yang Yu,Yilin Jiang,Zexuan Fei,Yiming Luo,Xingkai Song,Kaiyi Huang,Aimin Zhou,Xin Lin,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Data Evolution (ADE)框架，通过Observation-Variation-Selection (OVS)闭环过程和稳态准入机制作为质量棘轮，将合成监督数据组织为演化的数据快照，解决大语言模型在非可执行、上下文依赖目标下对齐困难、验证不可靠及噪声信号导致迭代退化的问题。在DEV300上内在胜率从50%提升至75.81%，外在胜率从55.20%提升至68.86%，盲评估中66.11%偏好进化后的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与人类中心目标的对齐在目标不可执行且上下文依赖时面临验证困难与可扩展监督受限的问题。虽然合成数据扩大了覆盖范围，但弱验证将瓶颈从生成转移到选择环节；噪声信号会破坏迭代优化的稳定性并导致静默回退（silent regressions），亟需一种数据为中心（data-centric）的解决方案来可靠地提升合成监督数据的质量。

Method: 提出Agentic Data Evolution (ADE)框架，将合成监督组织为不断演化的数据快照。ADE通过Observation-Variation-Selection (OVS)闭环过程改进数据快照，其中稳态准入机制（steady-state admission mechanism）充当质量棘轮（quality ratchet），以保守方式门控更新，确保跨轮次的持续改进。验证采用互补的双重策略：内在趋势追踪和外在事后训练评估。

Result: 在DEV300基准上，ADE将内在胜率从50%提升至75.81%，外在胜率从55.20%提升至68.86%，在多样化基准上表现一致。盲专家评估确认66.11%偏好于进化后的答案。性能提升横跨多种事后训练方法、模型规模和超出目标弱可验证教育目标的多种任务。

Conclusion: ADE作为一种数据为中心的合成监督演化框架，有效解决了弱验证环境下数据选择与迭代优化的难题，通过质量棘轮机制保证了持续改进而非退化，且具有良好的跨方法、跨模型规模和跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to human-centered objectives is difficult when targets are non-executable and context-dependent, limiting reliable verification and scalable supervision. Although synthetic data expands coverage, weak verification shifts the bottleneck from generation to selection. Noisy signals destabilize iterative refinement and can cause silent regressions. We propose Agentic Data Evolution (ADE), a data-centric framework that organizes synthetic supervision as evolving data snapshots. ADE improves data snapshots through a closed-loop Observation-Variation-Selection (OVS) procedure, where a steady-state admission mechanism acts as a quality ratchet that conservatively gates updates for sustained cross-round improvement. We validate these improvements through complementary intrinsic trend tracking and extrinsic post-training evaluation. On DEV300, ADE raises the intrinsic win rate from 50% to 75.81% and the extrinsic win rate from 55.20% to 68.86%, consistent performance gains across diverse benchmarks. Blind expert evaluation further confirms this, with a 66.11% preference for evolved answers. These gains extend across post-training methods, model scales, and tasks beyond the target weakly verifiable educational objectives. Resources are available at https://github.com/ZeroLoss-Lab/Agentic-Data-Evolution.

</details>


### [103] [What Reaches Expert Review? Representation, Structural Screening, and Candidate-Form Dependence in AI-Assisted Item Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23766)
*Christopher Brooks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两项关联的计算机模拟研究（涉及32,000个Big Five条目），追踪从语义表征到结构评估再到候选形式构建的全流程，发现全局指标看似稳定，但表征选择、结构约简和筛选策略会显著改变实际呈递给心理测量学家的条目内容与证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI辅助条目生成与专家评审之间，计算评估器通常被视为中立的技术预备步骤，但其决策（表征方式、结构约简、选择策略）实际决定了心理测量学家最终能收到的条目和证据，然而这一影响鲜被系统检验。

Method: 开展两项关联的in-silico研究，固定源条目总体（32,000个入选的Big Five条目），从语义表征出发，依次经过结构评估和候选形式构建，系统比较不同配置下的结果差异。

Result: 全局语义几何高度一致的表征配置在局部产生了显著差异：相同措辞获得不同构念证据、存活条目不同、目标属性可能在社群对应改善的同时消失；这些敏感性因生成源总体而异。最终评审边界上，两种资格策略虽填满每个可评估形式的所有内容单元，但措辞不同；不同嵌入配置下，包容性主要形式中位数仅共享40个条目中的6个。

Conclusion: 全局摘要的稳定性和形式的完整性掩盖了到达心理测量学家手中的内容的不稳定性。计算评估器并非生成与专业知识之间的中立基础设施，而是可检查、可修订的测量设计组成部分。

Abstract: Between AI-assisted item generation and expert review sits a computational evaluator whose decisions are usually treated as technical preliminaries. Yet representation, structural reduction, and selection policy determine which items and evidence psychometricians ever receive. Across two linked in-silico studies of 32,000 selected Big Five items, we followed fixed source populations from semantic representation through structural evaluation and candidate-form construction. Broad agreement in semantic geometry concealed consequential local differences: identical wording acquired different construct evidence, different items survived, and intended attributes could disappear even as community correspondence improved. These sensitivities also differed across generated source populations. At the final review boundary, both eligibility policies filled every content cell in every evaluable form, yet they presented different wording. Across embedding configurations, inclusive primary forms shared a median of only 6 of 40 items, reflecting the total downstream consequence of changing representation across structural evidence and ranking. The apparent stability of global summaries and complete forms therefore concealed instability in the content reaching psychometricians. The computational evaluator is not neutral infrastructure between generation and expertise; it is an inspectable and revisable part of measurement design.

</details>


### [104] [When Youth Enter The Chat: An Epistemic Shift in the Validation of LLM-Based Measures of Student Talk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23780)
*Liliana Santos-Deonizio,James Malamut,Ramón Martínez,Dorottya Demszky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前使用LLM大规模衡量学生课堂话语的做法，认为基于转录文本的测量缺乏语境且验证方法（成人专家标注、F1分数等）不足以确保对种族和语言边缘化青年有意义和公平。通过在一间8年级数学课堂中对多语言青年采用民族志方法（参与观察、访谈、焦点小组、成员检验）进行案例研究，发现学生对自身数学对话体验的理解与LLM测量结果存在不一致，学生质疑LLM分类和编码方案，凸显了让青年参与知识生产认知过程的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被越来越多地用于大规模测量学生话语（如发言动作、协作、话语平等），现有验证实践（与成人专家标注比较、使用保留评估集和F1分数）无法确保这些测量对教学有意义且公平，尤其对种族和语言边缘化的青年。作者认为转录文本使语言去语境化，而现有验证方法不足以捕捉课堂对话的复杂性和学生的真实体验，需要重新语境化并让青年参与研究过程。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，研究对象为8年级数学课堂中的多语言青年。研究者在四个焦点学生中运用多种民族志导向方法：参与观察（participant observations）、访谈（interviews）、焦点小组（focus groups）和成员检验（member checks），以重新语境化学生对话并将青年定位为与研究者及LLM对话中的认知权威。通过比较学生的自我解读与LLM测量结果来识别两者之间的错位。

Result: 研究发现，学生对其自身数学对话体验的理解与LLM基于转录文本的测量结果之间存在不一致（misalignments）。学生不仅对LLM的具体分类结果提出质疑，还质疑了用于测量其话语的编码方案本身，揭示了LLM测量无法捕捉学生自身对课堂对话的复杂理解和细微差别。

Conclusion: 让青年参与关于其自身体验的知识生产过程是必要的。共享认知权威（sharing epistemic authority）能够将青年的视角置于中心，为话语分析提供成人专家、研究者和LLM无法提供的关键细微差别。面向种族和语言边缘化青年的课堂话语测量需要在验证方法上做出根本性转变，从仅依赖成人专家和自动化指标转向纳入青年自身的认知参与。

Abstract: LLMs are being used increasingly to measure aspects of student discourse (e.g. talk moves, collaboration, equity of voice) at scale. Typically, LLM-based measures of student talk use transcriptions of classroom conversations that only include verbal contributions, which de-contextualize student language. Common practices for validating these measures include comparing outputs against expert annotations by adults, using held out evaluation sets and F1 scores. We argue that these approaches are insufficient to ensure that such measures are meaningful and equitable for teaching and learning, particularly for racially and linguistically marginalized youth. In order to center the youth whose talk is being analyzed, re-contextualizing these classroom conversations and engaging youth in the research process is necessary. Sharing epistemic authority with youth, ultimately, centers their point of view and adds crucial nuance to the analysis of their talk that adult experts, researchers, and LLMs cannot provide. In a case study of multilingual youth in one 8th-grade math classroom, we address the epistemic exclusion of youth by employing multiple ethnographically-oriented methods to re-contextualize student conversations and center youth as epistemic authorities in conversation with researchers and LLMs. We conducted participant observations, interviews, focus groups, and member checks with four focal students. Findings reveal that there were misalignments between students' interpretations of their own math talk experiences and the LLM-based measures of their talk. Students contested both the LLM classifications and the coding scheme used to measure their talk, highlighting the need for youth to be involved in the epistemic process of producing knowledge about their experiences.

</details>


### [105] [Inter-dimension Dependence for Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Open-Ended Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23783)
*Haoyuan Li,Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CorrGap指标来量化LLM裁判在评估开放文本时对非目标维度的依赖程度（即维度间依赖性），并发现这种依赖在LLM裁判中普遍存在。为缓解该问题，提出DimCheck方法，通过逐步去除CoT中无关证据来降低维度间依赖性，并在三个LLM和四个任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge被广泛用于评估开放文本生成质量，此类评估通常是多维度的（如相关性、流畅性、连贯性等）。一个可靠的LLM裁判应当独立评估每个目标维度，避免对其他维度产生依赖。然而现有研究缺乏量化这种维度间依赖性的方法，也没有有效的缓解策略。

Method: （1）提出CorrGap指标：通过比较不同文本分组中LLM预测分数与真实分数之间的相关性差异来量化维度间依赖性。（2）提出DimCheck方法：以逐步方式迭代地从LLM裁判生成的CoT（思维链）中去除与目标维度无关的证据，从而缓解维度间依赖性。此外，还探索了用较小的训练LLM来近似大型LLM执行DimCheck，以降低推理成本。

Result: 实验表明：维度间依赖性在LLM裁判中普遍存在；DimCheck方法能有效缓解维度间依赖性，并在三个LLM和四个任务上超越强基线；较小的训练LLM可以近似大型LLM在DimCheck中的表现，同时显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 维度间依赖是LLM裁判评估开放文本时的系统性缺陷，需要专门的量化和缓解手段。CorrGap提供了可靠的量化指标，DimCheck提供了有效的缓解方案，且该方法在效率上具有实际可行性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge methods are widely used for evaluating the quality of generated open-ended text. Such evaluations are generally multi-dimensional, since the error patterns in texts can be different for different dimensions. Therefore, reliable LLM judges should evaluate each target dimension independently. To quantify the extent to which LLM judges depend on non-target dimensions when evaluating a target dimension, i.e., inter-dimension dependence, we propose CorrGap. To measure this, CorrGap uses the difference in correlations between LLM-predicted scores and ground truth scores across different groups of texts. Using CorrGap, we show that inter-dimension dependence is pervasive across LLM judges in open-ended text evaluation tasks. To mitigate inter-dimension dependence, we propose DimCheck, a method that iteratively removes unrelated evidence from COTs generated by LLM judges in a step-wise way. We show that DimCheck mitigates inter-dimension dependence and outperforms strong baselines across three LLMs and four tasks. We also show that smaller trained LLMs can approximate larger LLMs in DimCheck, with much lower inference costs.

</details>


### [106] [Giga-Embeddings: Mixture-of-Experts Encoders for High-Throughput Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23806)
*Egor Kolodin,Egor Krasnoperov,Evgeniy Kosarev,Fyodor Minkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Giga-Embeddings，一个文本嵌入模型家族，包含稀疏10B参数的Mixture-of-Experts编码器（约1.8B活跃参数）、稠密3B编码器和蒸馏480M编码器。10B模型在英语、俄语、多语言和代码MTEB基准上均取得家族内最佳表现，在vLLM基准中实现114.5k tokens/s的吞吐量；480M模型在俄语MTEB上达到70.98分，超越FRIDA且参数减少42%。所有三个检查点均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本嵌入模型在检索质量和推理效率之间存在权衡，大型模型通常吞吐量较低，难以高效服务。作者旨在构建一个既能保持强检索质量又能实现高效服务的嵌入模型家族，覆盖不同计算和内存预算场景。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Experts架构构建稀疏10B参数编码器（每token约1.8B活跃参数），同时提供稠密3B编码器作为中间选项。对于紧凑的480M模型，使用维度无关的目标函数（dimension-agnostic objective）对齐教师和学生模型的相似度分布进行蒸馏训练，实现从大模型到小模型的知识迁移。

Result: 10B稀疏模型在英语、俄语、多语言和代码四个MTEB基准上均取得家族内最强聚合表现；在vLLM基准（1024 token输入）下吞吐量达114.5k tokens/s，比稠密3B模型高25%，是外部评估系统的1.56-2.65倍。480M蒸馏模型在俄语MTEB上达到70.98分，超越FRIDA且参数减少42%。

Conclusion: Giga-Embeddings通过MoE架构和蒸馏技术，成功在检索质量与推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，提供了从480M到10B的多档位模型选择，满足不同部署场景需求，并全部开源。

Abstract: We introduce Giga-Embeddings, a family of text embedding models designed to combine strong retrieval quality with efficient serving. Its largest member is a sparse 10B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts encoder with approximately 1.8B active parameters per token. Across English, Russian, multilingual, and code MTEB benchmarks, this model achieves the strongest aggregate performance within the family on all four evaluated suites. In our vLLM benchmark with 1024-token inputs, it processes 114.5k tokens per second, providing 25 percent higher throughput than the dense 3B model and 1.56-2.65x the throughput of the evaluated external systems. The family also includes a dense 3B encoder and a distilled 480M encoder for tighter compute and memory budgets. We train the compact model using a dimension-agnostic objective that aligns teacher and student similarity distributions. The resulting 480M model scores 70.98 on Russian MTEB, surpassing FRIDA while using 42 percent fewer parameters. We release all three model checkpoints.

</details>


### [107] [From Preferences to Principles: Rubric-Based Alignment for Grounded Knowledge Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23812)
*Aman Saini,Priyanshu Kumar,Eric Peng,Kai Yuan,Harsh Girase,Wanming Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于rubric（评分细则）的奖励框架，通过检索证据生成查询特定的多维度评分细则，为开放域问答的后训练提供细粒度监督信号，在三个评估轴上平均比指令微调基线提升6.5%，比扁平rubric变体提升4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域问答中设计有效的奖励信号具有挑战性，因为高质量回答需要同时满足多个方面的质量标准，而这些方面难以通过单一的标量目标函数来捕捉。现有的整体性（holistic）奖励机制无法提供足够精细的监督信号。

Method: 提出rubric-based奖励框架：1) 基于检索到的证据生成查询特定的rubric；2) 将rubric分解为多个质量维度（如组成、grounding、指令遵循等）；3) 在后训练阶段利用这些细粒度的多维评分细则提供监督信号。

Result: 在三个评估轴（组合性、grounding和指令遵循）上平均比指令微调基线提升6.5%，比扁平rubric变体提升4%，且在所有评估数据集上均有持续增益。基于证据的rubric改善了事实支撑，多维分解的rubric进一步提升了连贯性、组织性和查询需求遵循度。

Conclusion: 基于证据的多维rubric能为复杂的开放域问答提供更有效的奖励监督，表明细粒度、分解式的奖励设计优于整体性的标量奖励。

Abstract: Designing effective reward signals for open-domain question answering is challenging because high-quality responses must simultaneously satisfy multiple aspects of answer quality that are difficult to capture with a holistic scalar objective. We introduce a rubric-based reward framework that generates query-specific rubrics grounded in retrieved evidence and decomposed into multiple quality dimensions, providing fine-grained supervision during post-training. Averaged across three evaluation axes (composition, grounding, and instruction-following), our approach improves over the instruction-tuned baseline by 6.5% and over flat rubric variants by 4%, with consistent gains across all evaluation datasets. Conditioning rubrics on retrieved evidence improves factual support, while decomposing rubrics into quality-specific dimensions further improves coherence, organization, and adherence to query requirements. Our results show that grounded, multi-dimensional rubrics provide more effective reward supervision for complex open-domain question answering.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Static and Linear: What Attention Constraints Best Fit Human Reading Times?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23818)
*Lanni Bu,Xiulin Yang,Christian Clark,Alex Warstadt,Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种基于注意力机制的记忆约束方法对Transformer语言模型与人脑阅读行为拟合度的影响，发现内容敏感的注意力约束（而非距离约束）最接近人类阅读时间，且动态记忆课程下心理测量拟合与语法能力出现分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型被广泛用作人类语言处理的认知模型，但其注意力机制可无损访问全部上文，这与人类有限的记忆系统不符。作者假设在注意力机制中引入记忆约束可以提升模型对人类行为数据的拟合度，但此前研究仅孤立地探索单一约束，缺乏系统比较。

Method: 作者对多种基于注意力机制的记忆约束方法进行了系统比较，跨多个模型尺寸和训练语料库，同时评估模型对人类阅读时间的心理测量预测力（psychometric predictive power）以及语法能力。此外，还将静态约束（训练全程固定约束强度）与动态记忆课程（dynamic memory curricula）进行了对比。

Result: 对中间词元内容敏感的约束在人类阅读时间对齐上表现最佳，优于基于距离的约束。在动态记忆课程条件下，心理测量拟合度与语法能力之间出现分离现象，即两个指标不能同时最优。

Conclusion: Transformer无法作为"一刀切"的认知模型，其记忆约束的选择取决于研究目标（拟合人类行为还是追求语法能力），内容敏感的注意力约束是更优的认知建模策略。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widely used as models of human language processing, yet their attention mechanisms allow lossless access to the full preceding context, unlike the limited memory systems of humans. We hypothesize that installing memory constraints into transformers' attention mechanisms can improve their fit to human behavioral data. While previous work has explored individual constraints in isolation, we conduct a systematic comparison of multiple attention-based memory mechanisms across different model sizes and training corpora, evaluating both psychometric predictive power for human reading times and grammatical competence. We additionally compare static constraints, in which the constraint strength is fixed throughout training, to dynamic memory curricula. We find that constraints that are sensitive to the content of intervening tokens consistently achieve the highest alignment with human reading times, outperforming distance-based constraints. We observe a dissociation between psychometric fit and grammatical competence under dynamic memory curricula, suggesting that Transformers cannot serve as a one-size-fits-all cognitive model.

</details>


### [109] [Mitigating Exploration Bias in RL for Multi-Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23830)
*Mian Zhang,Yueqin Yin,Kaiyu He,Peilin Wu,Xinlu Zhang,Mingyuan Zhou,Zhiyu Zoey Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM指令跟随RL训练中的探索偏差问题（模型偏向简单指令），提出两个度量指标和两阶段框架（行为引导+稀缺性感知奖励），在三个可验证指令跟随基准上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练配方在提升LLM指令跟随能力时存在探索偏差，即当提示词中包含多条指令时，模型偏向于满足简单指令而忽视困难指令。这种偏差源于两点：1) 策略模型初始能力不足以对困难指令触发成功的探索；2) 现有RL训练采用累积奖励（满足指令的数量），对所有指令同等对待，使得模型只需满足简单指令就能获得相同奖励，从而产生偏置。

Method: 1) 提出两个指标来衡量指令跟随中的探索偏差；2) 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段为行为引导（Behavioral Bootstrapping），即在RL之前进行轻量级拒绝采样微调以激活困难指令；第二阶段为稀缺性感知奖励（Scarcity-Aware Rewards），即根据指令的经验稀缺性分配奖励权重，而非同等对待所有指令。

Result: 实验表明提出的偏差度量指标与模型性能高度相关；所提方法充分发挥了RL训练的潜力，最佳模型在三个可验证的指令跟随基准上显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 通过行为引导激活困难指令和稀缺性感知的奖励设计，可以有效缓解RL训练中的探索偏差，提升LLM在多指令场景下的指令跟随能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: RL has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing the instruction following capabilities of LLMs. While existing training recipes achieve substantial gains, we find that they suffer from exploration bias towards easy instructions when the training data has multiple instructions in a prompt. This bias is caused by two main reasons: 1) the policy model's initial ability to satisfy hard instructions is too low to trigger successful exploration during RL training, so the optimization is biased towards easy instructions; and 2) canonical RL training recipes typically employ a cumulative reward (the number of instructions fulfilled), treating all instructions equally, which biases the policy model towards fulfilling easy instructions to obtain the same amount of reward. To address these issues, we first propose two metrics to measure the exploration bias in instruction following and then introduce a two-stage framework to alleviate it: 1) Behavioral Bootstrapping, a lightweight rejection sampling fine-tuning stage before RL to activate hard instructions; and 2) Scarcity-Aware Rewards, a new RL reward function that assigns rewards to instructions based on their empirical scarcity. Experiments show that the proposed metrics are highly correlated with model performance, and our methods unleash the potential of RL training: our best models outperform the baselines by a significant margin across three verifiable instruction following benchmarks. We release codes at https://github.com/mianzhang/MulIF.

</details>


### [110] [Does Episodic Memory Help Close the Lexical Frequency Gap in Sensitivity to Syntactic Contrasts? A Test Using Retrieval-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23851)
*Jing Liu,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种假设：通过海马体情景记忆机制（以检索增强语言模型为实例）可以缩小语言模型在句法对比测试中对词汇频率的敏感性差距。实验表明检索增强确实缩小了高频和低频项目之间的性能差距，但未能完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语法知识及其经验测试被认为对词汇频率稳健，但基于神经网络的语法模型对词汇频率高度敏感。作者基于互补学习系统理论，假设海马体情景记忆机制可以帮助学习者处理罕见模式，从而使语法判断对词汇频率更具鲁棒性。

Method: 使用检索增强语言模型（具体为k近邻语言模型，将显式实例存储与参数化模型结合）作为情景记忆机制的实现。通过频率分层的句法对比测试集，比较普通语言模型与检索增强语言模型在高频和低频词汇项目上的性能差距，并控制结构信息与语义相似度对检索效果的贡献。

Result: 检索增强缩小了高频与低频项目之间的性能差距，且此效益在多种句法现象以及在不同规模预训练数据（儿童拟真和大规模数据）的模型中均一致。结构信息对有效检索至关重要，而语义相似度单独提供的帮助有限。然而频率差距只是被缩小而非完全消除。

Conclusion: 结果支持情景记忆机制可以部分补偿弱参数化表示、增强语言模型对词汇频率鲁棒性的假设。未来方向包括对检索实例进行优先重新加权、更好的结构信息表示与检索策略，以及灵活配置存储与检索机制来进一步改进语言模型中情景记忆的实现。

Abstract: Grammatical knowledge and how it is empirically tested are typically considered robust to the frequency of the lexical items in the expressions. However, neural network-based models of grammaticality exhibit high sensitivity to lexical frequency. We draw upon Complementary Learning Systems theory to test the hypothesis that robustness to lexical frequency can arise via a hippocampal episodic memory mechanism, which enables rapid encoding and retrieval of specific experiences and allows learners to leverage them when processing rare patterns. We use retrieval-augmented language models as an instantiation of such an episodic memory mechanism (specifically, $k$-nearest-neighbor language models that augment parametric models with explicit instance storage), and test whether this augmentation helps close the lexical frequency gap that vanilla language models exhibit in syntactic contrast tests. Using syntactic contrasts with frequency-stratified test items, we find that retrieval augmentation narrows the performance gap between high- and low-frequency items, consistent with episodic memory compensating for weak parametric representations. This benefit is consistent across different syntactic phenomena and across models pretrained on child-realistic and large-scale data. Additionally, we show that structural information is critical for effective retrieval, whereas semantic similarity alone provides little benefit. While these are promising proof-of-concept results supporting our hypothesis, the frequency gap is narrowed rather than fully closed. Based on our analyses, we propose preferential reweighting of retrieved instances, better representations and retrieval strategies for structural information, and flexible configurations of storage and retrieval as promising future directions for improving the implementation of episodic memory in language models.

</details>


### [111] [Names Can Hurt: Spotting Slopsquatting Risks Caused by Package Name Hallucinations in Local Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23897)
*Akash Raj,Sargam Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种两层检测器（确定性PyPI存在性检查+随机森林分类器）防御"slopsquatting"攻击——即利用代码生成大模型幻觉出的包名在PyPI上预先注册从而构成供应链攻击。检测器嵌入LangGraph状态机，支持升温重试与跨模型回退，在300个精选提示上76%运行产生无幻觉代码，用户研究显示满意度和采用意向高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成语言模型会虚构不存在的Python包名，攻击者可在PyPI预注册这些名字，将幻觉转化为供应链攻击（slopsquatting）。需要检测机制在生成侧阻断该风险。

Method: 两层检测器：第一层为确定性PyPI存在性检查；第二层为基于十个特征（包名及PyPI元数据）的随机森林分类器。两层间由导入名协调器（import name reconciler）桥接，处理如cv2对opencv-python的命名映射。整体嵌入LangGraph状态机，失败时以升温温度重试，多次失败后路由至更强的回退模型。

Result: 在300个精选提示上，76%运行产生无幻觉代码；28.7%主模型耗尽重试预算，其中模型内重试恢复约四分之一，跨模型回退再恢复剩余16.5%。四个发现：(1)一半被标记的幻觉是PyPI上已注册的低质量仿冒知名项目包，由分类器而非确定性层捕获；(2)幻觉率随提示对抗性从0-10%线性增至40-73%；(3)较弱的原始模型对10个直接诱饵中6个未经帮助即拒绝，指令微调提供基线防御；(4)主模型与回退同属一家族时约84%故障重演，需跨家族配对。用户研究(n=24)满意度4.4/5，21/24表示采纳意向。

Conclusion: 该两层检测器结合状态机重试/回退机制可有效缓解slopsquatting攻击，但需跨模型家族配对回退以克服同类失败传染问题。研究为代码生成供应链安全提供了实用的防御基线。

Abstract: When a code generating language model fabricates a Python package name, an adversary who has pre-registered that name on PyPI can convert that hallucination into a supply chain compromise. This event has been termed as 'slopsquatting'. We propose a two layer detector to counter this issue. The first layer performs a deterministic PyPI existence check. The second is a Random Forest classifier trained on ten features derived from the package name and its PyPI metadata. An import name reconciler bridges the two, resolving cases such as 'import cv2' versus 'pip install opencv-python' without a security bypass. The detector is embedded in a LangGraph state machine that retries at escalating temperatures and, on repeated failure, routes to a stronger fallback model. Across 300 curated prompts, the pipeline produces hallucination free code on 76% of runs. The primary exhausts its retry budget on 28.7%; intra model retries recover roughly a quarter of those, and cross model fallback recovers a further 16.5% of the remainder. Four findings have been observed. First, half of the flagged hallucinations are packages already registered on PyPI, as low quality lookalikes of well known projects, caught by the classifier rather than the deterministic layer (e.g., pil, faiss, tabula, haystack). Second, hallucination rate scales almost linearly with prompt adversariality, from 0 to 10% on routine coding to 40 to 73% on slopsquat baits. Third, the weaker primary refused 6 of 10 direct baits unaided, suggesting recent instruction tuning provides a baseline defense. Fourth, when primary and fallback share a model family, approximately 84% of primary failures recur on the fallback, motivating cross family pairing. A user study (n = 24) reports mean satisfaction 4.4 out of 5 and 21 of 24 stated adoption intent.

</details>


### [112] [Investigating Knowledge Transfer Across Interactive Dialogue Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23969)
*Filippo Momentè,Mir Nafis Sharear Shopnil,Andrea de Varda,Pavel Merinov,Raffaella Bernardi,Oswald Lanz,Alessandro Suglia,Alessandro Torcinovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究不同对话游戏之间的知识迁移能力，通过在clembench套件上微调LLM，利用二元整数优化程序推导任务迁移图，并计算任务向量分析模型间的相似性，发现部分游戏从迁移中获益多于微调，且视空间类游戏迁移效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话游戏需要复杂的认知技能（如理解规则、协调行动），而语言既是理解规则也是执行行动的接口。因此，作者假设在特定语言游戏上的训练可能增强对其他任务也相关的特定能力，从而探究跨对话游戏的知识迁移规律。

Method: 在clembench套件的多个对话游戏上微调LLM模型，进行两种分析：1) 使用Zamir等人(2018)的二元整数优化程序，以任务性能为主要指标构建任务迁移图；2) 为每个游戏计算任务向量（Ilharco et al., 2022），研究微调模型之间的相似性及其与任务迁移性的关系。

Result: 迁移图分析发现：部分游戏从迁移中获得的收益大于直接微调，且视空间家族（如探索类游戏）的迁移效果最好。任务向量分析发现：基于相似性的方法能捕捉游戏角色关系，但几乎无法反映迁移性模式，说明需要更复杂的度量指标。

Conclusion: 对话游戏间存在非对称的知识迁移，视空间类游戏是良好的迁移源；但基于任务向量相似性的简单度量不足以预测迁移性，需要设计更精细的指标来刻画任务间可迁移特性。

Abstract: Dialogue games represent a challenging setting where complex cognitive skills are required to accomplish tasks while coordinating with other players. Considering that language represents an interface for both understanding the game rules and executing actions, it is reasonable to assume that training on a specific language game will enhance specific capabilities that might be relevant for other tasks as well. Motivated by this rationale, in this paper, we investigate how knowledge transfers across different dialogue games. We study transferability by finetuning LLM models on games from the clembench suite (Chalamalasetti et al., 2023) and performing two analyses: i) we derive a task-transferability graph using a binary integer optimization program from Zamir et al. (2018), using task performance as the main metric; and ii) we compute task vectors (Ilharco et al., 2022) for each game to study similarities across finetuned models and their task transferability. In our first analysis, we find that some games benefit more from transfer than finetuning, and that the visuospatial family (e.g., exploration games) transfers best. With our task vector analysis instead, we find that similarity-based approaches capture game-role relationships but almost no transferability patterns, suggesting that more complex metrics are required.

</details>


### [113] [AgentSpec: Speculative Decoding for Batch Inference of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24004)
*Xin Wang,Ziming Miao,Yi Zhu,Hui Shen,Zhongwei Wan,Fan Yang,Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentSpec是一种面向LLM智能体应用的投机解码算法，通过结构隔离起草和冗余感知预算分配两种机制，解决了现有投机解码在批量推理下速度退化的问题，在五个工作负载和四个模型家族上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体应用响应时间长，投机解码是提升推理效率的有效手段，但现有投机解码算法在大批量场景下速度退化严重，限制了其在真实智能体应用中的部署。论文先对LLM智能体的投机解码进行系统分析，识别出两个主导退化因素：投机token的高拒绝率和动态token预算的低利用率。

Method: AgentSpec包含两个核心组件：(1) 结构隔离起草（structure-isolated drafting），将投机过程约束在智能体工作流的语义连贯片段内，减少无关语义路径的草稿生成，从而获得极低的token拒绝率；(2) 冗余感知预算分配（redundancy-aware budget allocation），利用智能体层面的信息更好地利用推理过程中动态空闲的token预算。

Result: 在vLLM中对五个不同工作负载、四个不同模型（来自四个LLM家族）进行评测，AgentSpec在所有设置下均优于现有最先进的投机解码方法。

Conclusion: 通过针对性解决大批量LLM智能体推理中投机解码的两个关键瓶颈（高拒绝率和预算利用率不足），AgentSpec在真实智能体工作负载上实现了显著优于现有方法的推理加速效果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agent applications often incur high response time. Speculative decoding is a promising solution to improve the inference efficiency of LLM agents without impacting generation quality. However, state-of-the-art speculative decoding algorithms exhibit substantial speed degradation under large batch sizes, limiting their effectiveness to deploy in real-world agent applications. In this work, we first present a systematic analysis of speculative decoding for LLM agents and identify two dominant factors of speedup degradation: high rejection rate of speculative tokens, and under-utilization of dynamic token budgets.B ased on these observations, we propose AgentSpec, a speculative decoding algorithm that addresses the limitations of existing methods for LLM agents. AgentSpec incorporates structure-isolated drafting that constrains speculation to semantically coherent segments of the agent workflow, reducing the drafts of irrelevant semantic paths and achieving an extremely low rejection rate. Moreover, AgentSpec adopts redundancy-aware budget allocation that exploits agent-level information to better utilize the dynamically-free token budget during the agent inference. We implement and evaluate AgentSpec on five different workloads and four different models from four different LLM families in vLLM. Our results demonstrate the superiority of AgentSpec over state-of-the-arts.

</details>


### [114] [SAGE: From Direct Answering to Evidence-Grounded Inference for Chinese Ancient Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24011)
*Yuchuan Wu,Xuan Luo,Yinglian Zhu,Meng Fang,Xiangyang Xue,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE是一个面向中国古代文献理解的证据驱动多智能体框架，通过规划、工具获取证据、声明级验证和有界重规划来替代传统LVLM的直接生成范式，在AncientDoc基准上超越直接回答基线，且9B规模的SAGE超过更大规模的单一大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中国古代文献理解需要复杂的视觉、语言和历史推理能力，而当前大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）通常采用不透明的单次生成范式，容易产生过度自信且缺乏依据的响应。这种直接生成方式难以满足古文理解中需要严谨证据支撑的要求。

Method: SAGE框架将古文理解重新定义为证据驱动的推理任务而非直接答案生成，其核心包含：（1）任务感知规划智能体；（2）工具介导的证据获取智能体；（3）声明级验证智能体；（4）有界重规划智能体。整个系统在受限共享状态运行时下运行，支持有界证据搜索、答案修订以及证据不足时的弃答机制。

Result: 在AncientDoc基准上的实验表明，SAGE在三个LVLM基座上均稳定优于匹配的直接回答基线。尤其值得注意的是，基于Qwen3.5-9B的SAGE在大多数评估指标上超越了更大的单一大规模LVLM，展示了结构化证据推理相对单纯扩大模型规模的优势。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了结构化、证据驱动的推理框架相比单纯依赖模型规模扩展更有效，为古文智能理解提供了一条可解释、可验证的新路径。多智能体协作的证据获取与验证机制是提升复杂领域视觉语言理解性能的关键。

Abstract: Chinese ancient document understanding demands complex visual, linguistic, and historical reasoning. Current Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) typically rely on an opaque, single-pass generation paradigm, often producing overconfident and weakly grounded responses. To address this, we propose SAGE, an evidence-grounded multi-agent framework that reformulates Chinese ancient document understanding as evidence-grounded inference rather than direct answer generation. SAGE coordinates specialized agents for task-aware planning, tool-mediated evidence acquisition, claim-level verification, and bounded replanning under a constrained shared-state runtime. This design supports bounded evidence seeking, answer revision, and abstention when grounding is insufficient. Experiments on the AncientDoc benchmark show that SAGE consistently outperforms matched direct-answering baselines across three LVLM backbones. Remarkably, SAGE with Qwen3.5-9B surpasses much larger monolithic LVLMs on most evaluated metrics, highlighting the importance of structured, evidence-grounded inference beyond model scaling.

</details>


### [115] [Curved Inference II: Sleeper Agent Geometry - Extending Interpretability Beyond Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24037)
*Rob Manson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自然主义的多轮上下文方法，在不依赖人工触发器或监督式后门插入的情况下，通过分析未归一化残差空间中的几何结构（语义表面积A'），实现对自然涌现的欺骗性对齐的检测，即使在线性探测方法失效时仍能通过测量精度揭示隐藏的几何签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Anthropic的Sleeper Agents研究显示人工后门可以通过线性探针以>99%准确率检测，但这种方法依赖线性可分性，这可能是后门插入的人为产物而非自然产生的欺骗性对齐的属性。自然训练中涌现的复杂欺骗行为不太可能产生这种方便的线性信号，因此需要一种不依赖人工触发器和监督标签的检测方法。

Method: 基于Curved Inference框架，引入语义表面积（semantic surface area, A'）这一新指标，在未归一化残差空间中捕捉意义构建的幅度与方向变化。通过多轮上下文窗口模拟自然的欺骗性推理，无需后门、标签或探针，采用LLM共识对模型输出进行分类，分析几何曲率、显著性与表面积的统计差异。

Result: 几何结构可靠地预测语义分类，在五种提示策略和两个模型家族中，表面积差异具有统计学显著性。关键的精度提升效果显著：某些策略从非显著（p=0.555）提升到显著（p=0.048），证明测量精度可以揭示被分类噪声掩盖的几何签名。

Conclusion: 复杂的推理会创建内在的几何模式，即使模型已学会抑制欺骗的线性指示器，这些模式仍然持续存在。推理本身的形状就编码了语义模式，这提供了一条可扩展的、无监督的检测路径，适用于线性方法失效的场景。

Abstract: This paper extends Anthropic's Sleeper Agents research [1], which showed artificial backdoors persist through safety training & can be detected by linear probes with >99% accuracy [2]. However, probe-based detection relies on linear separability that may be an artefact of backdoor insertion rather than a property of naturally occurring deceptive alignment. Sophisticated deceptive behaviours emerging through natural training are unlikely to produce such convenient linear signals.
  We introduce a naturalistic methodology using multi-turn context windows that simulates realistic deceptive reasoning without artificial triggers or supervised backdoor insertion. Rather than binary trigger-response patterns, we examine how semantic complexity emerges through gradual context development.
  Building on our Curved Inference framework, we analyse curvature, salience, & introduce semantic surface area (A'), a new metric of representational work capturing both the magnitude & directional change of meaning construction in unnormalised residual space. Without backdoors, labels, or probes, we apply this framework to naturalistic deceptive prompts & classify model outputs via LLM consensus.
  Geometric structure reliably predicts semantic classification, with statistically significant differences in surface area across five prompt strategies & two model families. Critically, measurement precision can reveal geometric signatures hidden by classification noise - some strategies improve from non-significant (p = 0.555) to significant (p = 0.048). This validates that sophisticated reasoning creates intrinsic geometric patterns that persist even when detection appears to fail, suggesting the shape of inference itself encodes semantic patterns regardless of whether models have learned to suppress linear indicators of deception - a scalable, unsupervised path for detection when linear methods fail.

</details>


### [116] [When Less Is More: An Empirical Study of Minimal Responses in Counseling Dialogues and the Behavior of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24080)
*Zhiyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了心理咨询对话中的"最小回应"（如简短的共情语句、后通道提示），发现其在人类收集的语料中常见，但在LLM生成的数据和模型中严重缺失，且现有LLM虽然能在明确指示下生成最小回应，却难以判断何时使用合适，评测框架也会低估这类回应的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理咨询对话系统和评价框架偏向显式、信息量丰富的回复，忽视了简短咨询师话语（如最小回应）在传达倾听、表达共情和鼓励来访者继续表达方面的交互价值，导致模型在真实咨询场景中表现不足。

Method: 开发了一个两阶段过滤方法：首先基于话语长度和内容进行筛选，然后使用大语言模型进行上下文验证；在多个心理咨询对话数据集上进行跨语言系统性分析，并设计人工策划的对话情境评估当前LLM生成最小回应的能力和判断时机的能力。

Result: 研究发现：（1）最小回应在人类收集的数据集中很常见，但在LLM生成的数据中大幅缺失；（2）强的商业LLM在被明确指示时能生成最小回应，但仍难以判断何时适用；（3）基于合成数据训练的心理咨询专用模型表现更差，倾向于生成更长、信息更丰富的回应；（4）LLM-based回应质量评估可能低估最小回应的价值，即使其在交互上恰当。

Conclusion: 心理咨询对话系统需要在生成能力和情境判断能力上同时提升，以更好地利用最小回应的交互价值；现有评测框架也需要改进，避免对信息量小的但交互上合适的回应进行系统性低估。

Abstract: In psychological counseling, effective support is not always delivered through long, information-rich responses. Minimal responses, such as backchannel cues and concise empathic statements, help convey attentive listening, express empathy, and encourage clients to continue expressing themselves. However, existing counseling dialogue systems and evaluation frameworks often favor explicit, content-rich replies, overlooking the interactional value of brief counselor utterances. This paper presents a systematic cross-lingual analysis of minimal responses across multiple counseling dialogue datasets. We develop a two-stage filtering method based on utterance length and content, followed by contextual verification using a large language model (LLM). Our analysis shows that minimal responses are common in human-collected datasets but substantially underrepresented in LLM-generated ones. We further evaluate current LLMs in manually curated dialogue contexts where human counselors used minimal responses. The results show that strong commercial LLMs are capable of generating minimal responses when explicitly instructed, but still struggle to determine when such responses are appropriate. Counseling-specific models trained on synthetic data perform particularly poorly, tending instead to produce longer and more information-rich responses. Moreover, LLM-based response-quality evaluation may undervalue minimal responses, even when they are interactionally appropriate.

</details>


### [117] [PARTAB: Partition-Aware Reasoning with Structured Evidence for Scalable Table Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24082)
*Md Mahadi Hasan Nahid,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了PARTAB框架，通过构建结构化证据接口，对表格进行语义分区和分层选择，显著提升了大语言模型在大规模、复杂表格上的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在表格推理中表现出强能力，但随着表格规模和复杂度增加，由于无关上下文干扰和难以定位推理所需的证据，模型性能会显著下降。现有方法要么在整个表格上推理，要么在单一缩减视图上推理，这两种方式都可能掩盖重要的行列关系，导致证据定位困难。

Method: PARTAB框架在LLM与表格之间构建一个结构化的证据接口。具体而言，它将与查询相关的证据表示为语义连贯的、以行链接的表格区域，并在组合所选证据进行答案生成之前，对列组和行级分区执行分层选择。核心步骤包括：1）对表格进行语义分区；2）对列组和行分区进行分层证据选择；3）组合选中证据用于答案生成。

Result: PARTAB在多个表格推理基准上（涵盖问答、事实验证和数值推理）一致优于全表提示和多种近期表格推理方法，在WikiTableQuestions和TabFact上取得了强劲性能，在数值推理上保持竞争力。额外分析表明，语义分区和定向证据选择改善了证据定位，大幅缩减了推理上下文，并在复杂表格上带来更大收益。

Conclusion: 结构化、分区感知的证据构建方法对可扩展的表格推理具有重要价值，证明了在面对大规模和复杂表格时，通过语义分区和分层证据选择可以有效提升大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in table reasoning, but their effectiveness degrades as tables grow in size and complexity due to irrelevant context and difficulty localizing the evidence required for reasoning. Existing approaches typically reason over either the full table or a single reduced view, which can still obscure important row-column relationships. We introducePARTAB (Partition-Aware Reasoning overTables), a framework that constructs a structured evidence interface between the LLM and the table. PARTAB represents query-relevant evidence as semantically coherent, row-linked table regions and performs hierarchical selection over column groups and row-level partitions before composing the selected evidence for answer generation. We evaluate PARTAB on multiple table reasoning benchmarks, covering question answering, fact verification, and numerical reasoning. PARTAB consistently improves over full-table prompting and several recent table reasoning methods, achieving strong performance on WikiTableQuestions and TabFact while remaining competitive on numerical reasoning. Additional analyses show that semantic partitioning and targeted evidence selection improve evidence localization, substantially reduce the reasoning context, and provide larger benefits on complex tables. These results demonstrate the value of structured, partition aware evidence construction for scalable table reasoning.

</details>


### [118] [MC-CXR: A Multi-Context Chest X-ray Benchmark for Context-Induced Disruption in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24118)
*Junhyeok Lee,Songsoo Kim,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多上下文胸部X光基准（MC-CXR），通过配对扰动来评估视觉语言模型在误导性上下文干扰下是否仍能保持正确的纯图像决策。评估10个VLM发现，图像精度必要但不充分，文本误导切换率高达45.6-78.1%，且存在显著的文本-视觉不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评估模型在孤立条件下是否正确回答问题，未评估当合理的上下文（如检索报告、初步笔记、既往影像）与图像冲突时，模型是否会被误导而放弃原本正确的纯图像决策。这在临床管线中至关重要，因为模型常被部署在有多源上下文的场景中。

Method: 构建MC-CXR基准：240个病例扩展为2,522个实例，通过配对扰动设计（固定当前图像和目标发现，同时提供匹配的可靠/误导性文本和既往CXR上下文），定义三个任务族和两个配对指标（switch-to-wrong rate和context-aligned error rate），并在标准化直接回答协议下评估10个跨开源通用、医学领域和闭源系统的VLM。

Result: 图像精度必要但不充分。误导性文本源的切换率均值为45.6-78.1%，误导性视觉源的切换率为35.7-61.7%。在切换的预测中，74.6%的文本上下文与误导标签一致，而视觉上下文仅为17.6%，差异达57.0个百分点（95% CI 50.9-62.8），显示出显著的文字-视觉不对称性。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在临床场景中易受误导性文本上下文的系统性干扰而翻转正确决策，文本上下文比视觉上下文具有更强的误导效应。这提示需要开发对上下文扰动更鲁棒的模型，并应在其实际临床部署前评估上下文鲁棒性，而不只是孤立的图像精度。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in clinical pipelines where a chest X-ray is interpreted alongside retrieved reports, preliminary notes, or prior imaging. Existing benchmarks measure whether models answer correctly in isolation, but not whether they preserve a correct image-only decision when plausible context conflicts with the image. We introduce Multi-Context Chest X-ray (MC-CXR), a benchmark of 240 cases expanded into 2,522 instances that isolates context-induced disruption through paired perturbation. Each case fixes the current image and target finding while presenting matched reliable and misleading context across text and prior CXR, with visual overlays where available. MC-CXR defines three task families and two paired metrics, the switch-to-wrong rate and the context-aligned error rate. We evaluate ten VLMs spanning open-source general, medical-domain, and closed-source systems. Image-only accuracy is necessary but insufficient. Mean switch rates range from 45.6-78.1% across misleading textual sources and 35.7-61.7% across misleading visual sources. Among switched predictions, 74.6% align with the misleading label for text versus 17.6% for visual context, a 57.0-point gap (95% CI 50.9-62.8). This text-visual asymmetry is observed under the standardized direct-answer protocol. The dataset is available on PhysioNet.

</details>


### [119] [TrustDABench: Benchmarking Reliability and Robustness of LLMs for Structured Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24145)
*Boshen Shi,Yize Liu,Chen Zhao,Ce Chi,Zhendong Wang,Xing Wang,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL
