One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [379] [StarHarness: Evolving Harnesses with Stratified Search for Enterprise Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24804)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Denis Akhiyarov,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar,Patrice Bechard,Sridhar Nemala,Sagar Davasam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StarHarness是一个在保持模型权重不变的前提下，为具体环境自动进化agent harness（提示词、任务框架、工具接口、技能、MCP后端、子代理结构及代理循环配置）的框架。通过在ITBench SRE、EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM和AutomationBench Finance三个基准上，经4-12次接受的环境变更，全benchmark性能提升20-35个百分点，且收益能泛化到未见任务并跨GPT/Qwen模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具丰富的企业任务中，模型与环境之间存在持续的不匹配（model-environment mismatch），这种不匹配导致即便模型能力强大，默认harness下任务表现仍受限于接口、提示和工具使用方式等框架层面的不足。现有方法通常需要微调或替换模型，缺乏一种保持模型固定、仅针对环境演化agent框架的实用方案。

Method: StarHarness通过三个关键设计实现环境特定的harness进化：(1) 依据基线失败行为对任务进行分层，构建紧凑的进化池；(2) 将提议者可见的搜索任务与提议者隐藏的选择任务分离，防止过拟合进化信号；(3) 预留held-out任务用于评估泛化性。进化过程通过迭代生成并接受对harness各组件（提示、工具接口、技能、MCP provider、子代理结构、agent循环配置等）的修改。

Result: 在ITBench SRE、EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM和AutomationBench Finance三个基准上，harness进化在每环境仅4-12次被接受的改动后，将完整benchmark性能提升20-35个百分点。收益在排除于进化过程之外的任务上依然保持，且无需重新进化即可在GPT和Qwen模型家族间迁移。轨迹分析显示改进源于接口修复、环境约定和压缩搜索的操作知识，具体表现为误报诊断减少和轨迹长度缩短。

Conclusion: StarHarness提供了一种实用且高效的方式来解决工具密集型企业任务中的模型-环境不匹配问题，将优化焦点从模型权重转移到harness层，以极少的改动数量即可获得显著且可泛化的性能提升，为agent-环境适配提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We present StarHarness, a framework for evolving environment-specific agent harnesses while keeping model weights fixed. The evolved harness can include prompt and task framing, tool interfaces, skills, MCP-backed providers, subagent structure, and agent-loop configuration. StarHarness constructs a compact evolution pool by stratifying tasks according to baseline failure behavior, separates proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, and reserves held-out tasks for evaluating generalization. Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, harness evolution improves full-benchmark performance by 20-35 percentage points over the default harness after 4-12 accepted changes per environment. These gains persist on tasks excluded from evolution and transfer without re-evolution across GPT and Qwen model families. Trace analysis links the improvements to interface repairs, environment conventions, and operational knowledge that compresses search, with fewer false-positive diagnoses and shorter trajectories in several settings. StarHarness therefore offers a practical way to reduce persistent model-environment mismatch in tool-rich enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [380] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种严格因果的流式视频异常检测器，其固定大小状态以O(1)时间和内存逐帧更新，无需裁剪或缓冲，并推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟及可捕捉最短异常之间的闭式关系，在Apple M3 Pro硬件上实现了0.74-0.77毫秒/帧（超1300 FPS）的端到端延迟，但精度略低于非因果SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba风格状态空间模型的视频异常检测方法仍依赖内部缓冲裁剪或窗口，缺乏时间记忆与检测延迟之间关系的理论解释，且仅通过GPU吞吐量而非实际目标边缘硬件进行效率评估，这些问题需要被解决。

Method: 提出严格因果的流式异常检测器，其时间核心是带输入和状态相关衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，通过冻结视觉骨干上的因果下一嵌入预测进行自监督训练。推导了衰减谱与检测延迟及最短可检测异常之间的闭式关系，并在Apple M3 Pro硬件上直接测量端到端延迟和吞吐量。

Result: UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上帧级AUC分别达到67.9%和70.2%，略低于非因果SSM基线；在Apple M3 Pro上每帧延迟0.74-0.77毫秒（超1300 FPS）；理论预测的稳定延迟（57-59帧）远高于实测检测延迟（1.6和18.4帧），表明事件边界门而非基础衰减主导响应性；消融显示门控贡献与数据集大小相关，在小数据集Ped2上损害精度，在大数据集Avenue上提升精度。

Conclusion: 提出的严格因果流式异常检测器在效率上具有显著优势，理论框架揭示了边界门在响应性中的核心作用，但精度方面落后于非因果基线，需缩小精度差距并将评估扩展到更大的第三个基准数据集。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [381] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES-PK（约束候选实体选择）框架，用于LLM-based KGQA场景，通过轻量级符号约束（类型、关系、排除）对LLM生成的候选答案进行验证和筛选，无需构建可执行的语义解析结果。针对知识图谱不完整问题，采用三值约束语义（满足、违反、未知），在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上实验表明可提升精确率同时保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based KGQA方法存在两难：完全语义解析（如SPARQL）对复杂模式或真实KG的不完整性脆弱且不鲁棒；而LLM在KG上的推理和答案生成虽然更鲁棒但缺乏形式化保证。因此需要一种既能利用符号约束提供形式化验证，又不需要完整语义解析的中间路径。

Method: 提出CES-PK问题形式化，该框架由LLM系统生成候选答案，然后利用从问题中提取的轻量级符号约束（包括类型约束、关系约束和排除约束）对候选进行验证。采用三值约束语义（satisfied, violated, unknown）处理KG不完整性问题，避免在开放世界假设下错误拒绝候选答案。

Result: 在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上的实验表明：通过过滤无效候选可提升精确率；由于保留未被明确违反约束的候选，召回率得到保持；满足约束所提供的正面符号证据可对剩余候选进行排序。

Conclusion: CES-PK方法在LLM-based KGQA中提供了一种介于完全语义解析和纯LLM推理之间的折中方案，利用轻量级符号约束进行答案验证，既增强了形式化保证，又避免了语义解析的脆弱性，适用于知识图谱不完整的真实场景。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [382] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的双维度自动化试题相似性分析框架（AISA），通过结构化分解与语义关联性两个维度来捕捉试题间非预期的内容冗余，并在心理测量验证和计算机化自适应测试（CAT）仿真中展示了其对传统文本相似度指标的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估的快速扩展与自动试题生成的普及，使得试题间非预期内容冗余（如措辞或情境框架的重复）日益严重，而BLEU、余弦相似度等传统指标难以同时捕捉驱动感知冗余的结构层与语义层复杂性，亟需更精细的相似性分析框架。

Method: 构建AISA双维度框架：一是结构化分解（Structured Decomposition），解析试题的结构组成；二是语义关联性（Semantic Relatedness），度量语义层面的关联程度，两者均由LLM驱动实现。随后进行心理测量学验证，并通过在CAT中引入基于LLM的相似性约束进行模拟实验，与传统指标约束进行对比。

Result: LLM衍生的相似性指标与构念无关的局部依赖性指标更吻合，产生更一致的试题参数分组；在CAT仿真中，基于LLM的相似性约束改善了估计稳定性并降低了偏差，仅以极小的效率代价为代价，优于传统指标的约束。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AISA在题库规模化维护、内容感知的测试组装以及对经验敏感的适应性测试中具有显著潜力，可为多种评估情境提供支持。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [383] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedV-KGQA框架，用于在垂直分区（各组织共享实体但拥有不相交的关系集合）的多知识图谱场景下进行多跳推理问答。通过本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入实现数据隔离，无需集中式图访问或运行时跨组织通信，在三类基准上评估12种配置，性能接近集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权约束分散在不同组织间。集中式系统无法回答所需事实分散在垂直分区数据孤岛中的多跳问题，因此需要一种既能保护数据隐私又能进行跨组织多跳推理的方案。

Method: FedV-KGQA结合两种核心机制：(1) 本地图增强与知识图谱嵌入，确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各自的数据孤岛，建立结构化数据边界；(2) 主题实体锚定机制，将问题锚定到正确的图邻域中，无需运行时跨组织通信即可完成推理。

Result: 在三个基准上评估12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现强劲，性能接近集中式方法，能够泛化到3跳推理，并且对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA有效解决了垂直分区知识图谱下的多跳问答难题，在保证数据主权和隐私边界的前提下实现了接近集中式的推理性能，为联邦学习与知识图谱问答的交叉领域提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [384] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPO++，通过修正组相对强化学习中advantage的标准量化方式（在action-token measure下标准化终端结果优势，而非简单的轨迹中心化），并重新组织prompt证据的生成顺序，在ALFWorld和Math-TIR上显著提升在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对强化学习方法（如GRPO）依赖同一prompt的sibling rollouts，导致长而可变工具使用轨迹的高昂成本。SPO虽通过持久prompt级价值估计去除了该依赖，但其"每条轨迹白化一个advantage"的做法在数学上并未中心化actor实际消费的token加权量，造成优化偏差。

Method: SPO++提出两点改进：(1) 在action-token measure下标准化terminal-outcome advantages，修复轨迹级白化与token级actor损失之间的不匹配；(2) 按照生成prompt证据的策略事件顺序（而非学习者接收顺序）组织数据。核心组件为action-token measure归一化。

Result: 在ALFWorld两个模型规模和Math-TIR任务上与SPO进行匹配实验，SPO++在在线学习效率上全面优于SPO。配对消融实验表明action-token measure归一化是最强的贡献组件。

Conclusion: SPO++通过修正advantage的标准化度量与数据组织方式，在无需sibling rollouts的前提下提升了工具使用场景下的在线强化学习效率，验证了token级归一化对于策略优化的重要性。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [385] [Orientation in Extended Position-Based Dynamics: Application to Rigid Bodies and Cosserat Rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23606)
*Samuel Tobin,Caleb Rucker*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出将李群/李代数理论融入扩展位置动力学（XPBD），为3D旋转提供统一框架，用于表达旋转约束、插值和微分。该框架显著提升了刚体约束的动态一致性（相比最先进方法提升超过10^4倍），并自然扩展到有限元Cosserat杆的流行上插值，线性单元优于传统链式刚体离散化，高阶基函数提供更平滑解和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XPBD中旋转自由度需要在3D旋转的非线性流形上进行计算，现有方法缺乏统一、干净的数学框架来表达旋转约束、插值和微分，导致刚体约束的动态一致性差以及Cosserat杆离散化精度不足。

Method: 利用李群/李代数理论构建统一框架：(1) 推导显式的李理论约束公式及其梯度用于刚体模拟；(2) 在流形上进行节点旋转插值，将框架扩展到有限元Cosserat杆。

Result: 刚体约束的动态一致性相比最先进方法提升超过10^4倍；线性有限元在Cosserat杆模拟中优于传统链式刚体离散化；高阶基函数带来更平滑的数值解和更快的收敛速度；在多种大变形和接触场景中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 李理论为XPBD中旋转动力学提供了简洁而统一的数学框架，既能改善刚体模拟精度，又能自然支撑高阶有限元Cosserat杆，展现出良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Rotational degrees of freedom in Extended Position-Based Dynamics (XPBD) require computations on the nonlinear manifold of 3D rotations. We show that Lie theory provides a clean, unified framework for expressing rotations, constraints, interpolation, and differentiation in XPBD, enabling both improved rigid-body constraints and higher-order finite-element Cosserat rods. We derive explicit Lie-theoretic constraint formulations and their gradients for rigid-body simulation, improving the dynamic consistency of constrained rigid-body simulations in XPBD by a factor of over $10^4$ compared to the state-of-the-art. Our framework naturally extends to finite-element Cosserat rods by enabling on-manifold interpolation of nodal rotations. Linear finite elements outperform the conventional chain-of-rigid-bodies discretization, while higher-order basis functions provide even smoother solutions and faster convergence. Utility is demonstrated in a variety of examples with large deformations and contact.

</details>


### [386] [A Three-Parameter Binary Subdivision Scheme for Shape-Controlled Curve Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23637)
*Rabia Hameed,Jihad Younis,Maryam Salem Alatawi,Tahreem Farman,Hafiza Sana Mukhtiar*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三参数9点二进制逼近细分格式，通过加权组合7点Lagrange和7点B-spline细分格式的细化规则构建，利用三个独立设计控制参数调整曲线几何特征，建立了可统一表示的细分格式族，并对其支撑、连续性、端点规则和Gibbs振荡行为进行了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状控制的曲线设计在计算机辅助几何设计、计算机图形学和工程应用中具有基础性作用。现有细分格式缺乏灵活的设计控制参数，难以满足不同应用场景对曲线几何形状的多样化调节需求，因此需要一种兼具灵活几何构造与良好数学性质的细分格式家族。

Method: 通过7点Lagrange和7点B-spline细分格式的细化规则进行加权组合，利用对应细化点之间的位移向量构造合成向量，并通过三个独立的设计控制参数组合相邻位移向量，从而推导出新的细化规则，形成统一的二进制细分格式族。通过施加特定参数约束可得到两个代表性的子格式。

Result: 建立了新三参数9点二进制逼近细分格式族的理论框架，确定了格式族的支撑区间、连续性阶数、开放多边形的端点规则以及Gibbs振荡行为；图形示例验证了设计控制参数对极限曲线的影响。

Conclusion: 该细分框架因其灵活的几何构造、直观的参数化和良好的数学性质，为设计控制的曲线设计提供了灵活有效的工具，在计算机辅助几何设计及相关工程应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Shape-controlled curve design plays a fundamental role in computer-aided geometric design, computer graphics, and engineering applications. In this paper, we present a novel three-parameter 9-point binary approximating subdivision scheme constructed by a weighted combination of the refinement rules of the 7-point Lagrange and 7-point B-spline subdivision schemes. The proposed construction employs displacement vectors between the corresponding refinement points of the parent schemes and constructs resultant vectors by combining neighbouring displacement vectors through three independent design-control parameters. These resultant vectors are subsequently used to derive the refinement rules, thereby yielding a unified family of binary subdivision schemes with adjustable geometric characteristics while preserving the approximating nature of the refinement process. Two representative sub-schemes are derived as special cases by imposing suitable constraints on the design-control parameters. Theoretical investigations establish the support, continuity, endpoint rules for open polygons, and Gibbs oscillation behavior of the proposed family, while graphical examples demonstrate the influence of the design-control parameters on the generated limit curves. Owing to its flexible geometric construction, intuitive parameterization, and favorable mathematical properties, the proposed subdivision framework provides a flexible and effective framework for design-controlled curve design and a valuable tool for computer-aided geometric design and related engineering applications.

</details>


### [387] [ExMesh++: From Multi-View Images to Relightable UV-PBR Mesh Assets via Topology-Adaptive Reconstruction and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24109)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ExMesh++是一个多视图重建框架，通过两阶段流水线直接从多视图图像重建可重光照、可编辑的UV-PBR网格资产，获得具有良好拓扑、有效UV参数化和显式PBR材质贴图的网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表面重建方法（隐式场、高斯图元等）输出中间表示，转换成最终网格资产需要表面提取和纹理烘焙；逆渲染方法将材质和光照与神经场或点基元绑定，而非最终网格；联合优化几何、材质和光照时变量间易相互补偿导致分解模糊。这些限制阻碍了直接获得可编辑、可重光照的网格资产。

Method: ExMesh++采用两阶段流水线：(1) 第一阶段通过自适应顶点分裂与合并细化显式网格几何和拓扑，并在拓扑变化时保持UV一致性；(2) 第二阶段固定网格-UV载体，结合环境光照优化UV空间中的PBR贴图，并通过次级射线追踪建模单次弹射的漫反射间接光照，使用共享的UV-PBR材质。

Result: 实验表明ExMesh++在几何精度上具有竞争力，展现出较强的重光照性能，且导出的资产可直接用于标准DCC工作流。

Conclusion: ExMesh++通过显式网格与UV-PBR材质空间的解耦优化，在稳定载体上联合优化材质与环境光照并建模间接光照，解决了重建资产直接可用性的问题，实现了竞争性的几何精度和强重光照效果。

Abstract: Multi-view reconstruction extends beyond surface recovery to editable and relightable mesh assets. Such assets require well-formed topology, valid UV parameterization, and explicit PBR material maps. Existing surface reconstruction approaches optimize implicit fields, Gaussian primitives, or other intermediate representations. Converting them into such assets often requires surface extraction and texture baking. Inverse-rendering methods estimate materials and illumination, yet these components often remain tied to neural fields or point-based primitives rather than the final mesh. Joint optimization of geometry, materials, and lighting may also allow these variables to compensate for one another, leading to ambiguous decomposition. To address these limitations, we present ExMesh++, a staged framework for reconstructing relightable UV-PBR mesh assets from multi-view images. The first stage refines explicit mesh geometry and topology through adaptive vertex splitting and merging, while maintaining UV consistency as the topology changes. The second stage fixes the resulting mesh-UV carrier and optimizes UV-space PBR maps together with environment lighting. Building on this stable carrier, ExMesh++ models one-bounce diffuse indirect illumination through secondary-ray tracing with shared UV-PBR materials. Experiments demonstrate competitive geometry accuracy, strong relighting performance, and direct usability of the exported assets in standard DCC workflows.

</details>
